     1	"""Top-level application: auth, screen registry, navigation, shutdown."""
     2	import os
     3	import sqlite3
     4	import tkinter as tk
     5	from tkinter import ttk, messagebox
     6	
     7	from database import migrations, schema
     8	from database.connection import DB_PATH
     9	from screens.login import LoginDialog
    10	from screens.sales import SalesScreen
    11	from screens.products import ProductsScreen
    12	from screens.reports import ReportsScreen
    13	from screens.history import HistoryScreen
    14	from screens.users import UsersScreen
    15	from screens.deleted_sales import DeletedSalesScreen
    16	from utils import auth, backup, i18n
    17	from utils.i18n import t
    18	
    19	
    20	def _ensure_database() -> None:
    21	    if not os.path.exists(DB_PATH):
    22	        schema.create_tables()
    23	        return
    24	    conn = sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH)
    25	    try:
    26	        cur = conn.cursor()
    27	        cur.execute("SELECT name FROM sqlite_master "
    28	                    "WHERE type='table' AND name='products'")
    29	        if not cur.fetchone():
    30	            schema.create_tables()
    31	    finally:
    32	        conn.close()
    33	
    34	
    35	def _setup_ttk_style(root: tk.Tk) -> None:
    36	    style = ttk.Style(root)
    37	    try:
    38	        style.theme_use("clam")
    39	    except tk.TclError:
    40	        pass
    41	    style.configure("Treeview", rowheight=26, font=i18n.ui_font(9))
    42	    style.configure("Treeview.Heading", font=i18n.ui_font(9, "bold"))
    43	    style.configure("TNotebook.Tab",
    44	                    font=i18n.ui_font(9, "bold"), padding=[10, 3])
    45	
    46	
    47	# (screen name, required permission, screen class)
    48	_SCREEN_BUILDERS = (
    49	    ("sales",    "sell",               SalesScreen),
    50	    ("reports",  "view_reports",       ReportsScreen),
    51	    ("history",  "view_history",       HistoryScreen),
    52	    ("products", "manage_products",    ProductsScreen),
    53	    ("users",    "manage_users",       UsersScreen),
    54	    ("deleted",  "view_deleted_sales", DeletedSalesScreen),
    55	)
    56	
    57	_OPEN_ORDER = ("sales", "history", "reports", "products", "users", "deleted")
    58	
    59	
    60	class StockManager:
    61	    def __init__(self):
    62	        self.root = tk.Tk()
    63	        self.root.withdraw()
    64	        self.user = None
    65	        self.screens: dict[str, object] = {}
    66	        self.current_screen: str | None = None
    67	
    68	        _ensure_database()
    69	        migrations.ensure_auth_schema()
    70	        _setup_ttk_style(self.root)
    71	        backup.start_auto_backup()
    72	
    73	        if not self._do_login():
    74	            self.root.destroy()
    75	            return
    76	        if not self._build_screens():
    77	            self.root.destroy()
    78	            return
    79	
    80	        self._open_first_available()
    81	        self.root.deiconify()
    82	        self.root.protocol("WM_DELETE_WINDOW", self.on_closing)
    83	
    84	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    85	    # Login / build / teardown
    86	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    87	    def _do_login(self) -> bool:
    88	        login = LoginDialog(self.root)
    89	        self.user = login.show()
    90	        return bool(self.user)
    91	
    92	    def _build_screens(self) -> bool:
    93	        self.root.title(t("app.window_title",
    94	                           username=self.user["username"],
    95	                           role=t(f"role.{self.user['role']}")))
    96	        self.root.geometry("1400x850")
    97	        self.root.minsize(1100, 700)
    98	
    99	        for name, permission, cls in _SCREEN_BUILDERS:
   100	            if auth.can(self.user, permission):
   101	                self.screens[name] = cls(self.root, self.switch_screen,
   102	                                          self.user, self.logout)
   103	
   104	        if not self.screens:
   105	            messagebox.showerror(t("msg.error"), t("startup.no_screen"))
   106	            return False
   107	        return True
   108	
   109	    def _open_first_available(self) -> None:
   110	        for name in _OPEN_ORDER:
   111	            if name in self.screens:
   112	                self.current_screen = name
   113	                self.screens[name].show()
   114	                return
   115	
   116	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   117	    # Navigation
   118	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   119	    def switch_screen(self, screen_name: str) -> None:
   120	        if screen_name not in self.screens:
   121	            messagebox.showwarning(t("msg.access_denied"),
   122	                                   t("startup.permission_denied"))
   123	            return
   124	        if self.current_screen and self.current_screen in self.screens:
   125	            self.screens[self.current_screen].hide()
   126	        self.screens[screen_name].show()
   127	        self.current_screen = screen_name
   128	
   129	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   130	    # Logout
   131	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   132	    def logout(self) -> None:
   133	        if not messagebox.askyesno(t("msg.confirmation"), t("logout.confirm")):
   134	            return
   135	
   136	        # Best-effort backup before we tear everything down.
   137	        try:
   138	            backup.do_backup()
   139	        except Exception as e:
   140	            print(f"Backup on logout failed: {e}")
   141	
   142	        # Destroy every screen's frame.
   143	        for screen in self.screens.values():
   144	            try:
   145	                screen.hide()
   146	                screen.frame.destroy()
   147	            except Exception:
   148	                pass
   149	        self.screens.clear()
   150	        self.current_screen = None
   151	        self.user = None
   152	
   153	        # Hide the main window, show login again.
   154	        self.root.withdraw()
   155	        if not self._do_login():
   156	            self.root.destroy()
   157	            return
   158	        if not self._build_screens():
   159	            self.root.destroy()
   160	            return
   161	
   162	        self._open_first_available()
   163	        self.root.deiconify()
   164	
   165	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   166	    # Shutdown
   167	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   168	    def on_closing(self) -> None:
   169	        try:
   170	            backup.do_backup()
   171	        except Exception as e:
   172	            print(f"Backup on exit failed: {e}")
   173	        self.root.destroy()
   174	
   175	    def run(self) -> None:
   176	        self.root.mainloop()

FILE: CONTRIBUTING.md

     1	# Contributing
     2	
     3	Thanks for considering a contribution! 🎉
     4	
     5	## Setup
     6	
     7	```bash
     8	git clone https://github.com/<your-username>/stock-manager.git
     9	cd stock-manager
    10	python -m venv .venv && source .venv/bin/activate
    11	pip install -r requirements.txt
    12	python main.py
    13	```
    14	
    15	## Ground rules
    16	
    17	1. **Open an issue first** for anything bigger than a bug fix.
    18	2. **One concern per PR** — keep diffs focused and reviewable.
    19	3. **Don't break i18n.** Every user-facing string must go through `t("key")`
    20	   and exist in all three languages (`en`, `fr`, `ar`) in `utils/i18n.py`.
    21	4. **Never commit** `stock.db`, `backups/`, `reports/`, or `.idea/`.
    22	5. **Don't drop tables in `create_tables()`** — it's destructive. Migrate instead.
    23	
    24	## Code style
    25	
    26	- PEP 8, 4-space indent, ~88 cols.
    27	- Type hints welcome but not required.
    28	- Keep SQL in `database/db_operations.py` — screens should not run raw SQL.
    29	
    30	## Commit messages
    31	
    32	Use [Conventional Commits](https://www.conventionalcommits.org/):
    33	
    34	```
    35	feat(sales): add quantity discount
    36	fix(products): crash when photo path is missing
    37	docs(readme): add demo GIF
    38	```
    39	
    40	## Reporting bugs
    41	
    42	Please include:
    43	- OS + Python version
    44	- Steps to reproduce
    45	- Expected vs. actual behaviour
    46	- Traceback if available

FILE: database/connection.py

     1	"""SQLite connection factory — every read/write goes through here."""
     2	import os
     3	import sqlite3
     4	
     5	DB_PATH = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(__file__)), "stock.db")
     6	
     7	
     8	def get_connection() -> sqlite3.Connection:
     9	    conn = sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH, timeout=10)
    10	    conn.execute("PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL")
    11	    conn.execute("PRAGMA foreign_keys=ON")
    12	    return conn

FILE: database/errors.py

     1	"""Domain exceptions. Screens translate these with `t(e.key, **e.kwargs)`."""
     2	
     3	
     4	class DomainError(Exception):
     5	    """Base class. `key` is an i18n key; `kwargs` feed `str.format`."""
     6	    key = "msg.error"
     7	    def __init__(self, **kwargs):
     8	        self.kwargs = kwargs
     9	        super().__init__(self.key)
    10	
    11	
    12	class BarcodeExists(DomainError):
    13	    key = "err.barcode_exists"
    14	    def __init__(self, barcode):
    15	        super().__init__(barcode=barcode)
    16	
    17	
    18	class ProductNotFound(DomainError):
    19	    key = "err.product_not_found"
    20	
    21	
    22	class SaleNotFound(DomainError):
    23	    key = "err.sale_not_found"
    24	
    25	
    26	class SaleAlreadyDeleted(DomainError):
    27	    key = "err.sale_already_deleted"
    28	
    29	
    30	class SaleNotDeleted(DomainError):
    31	    key = "err.sale_not_deleted"
    32	
    33	
    34	class NoActiveSales(DomainError):
    35	    key = "err.no_active_sales"
    36	
    37	
    38	class NoSales(DomainError):
    39	    key = "err.no_sales"
    40	
    41	
    42	class NoDeletedSales(DomainError):
    43	    key = "err.no_deleted_sales"
    44	
    45	
    46	class InsufficientStock(DomainError):
    47	    key = "err.insufficient_stock"
    48	    def __init__(self, available, required=None):
    49	        super().__init__(available=available, required=required)

     1	"""Database package.
     2	
     3	Screens import the flat shim:
     4	
     5	    from database import db
     6	
     7	Nothing is imported here on purpose, so each submodule can be imported
     8	independently and errors point at the real file.
     9	"""

=================================================================
FILE: database/migrations.py

     1	"""Idempotent migrations run at every startup."""
     2	from utils import auth as _auth   # hashed here, but keeps the table local
     3	from .connection import get_connection
     4	
     5	
     6	def _column_exists(cur, table: str, column: str) -> bool:
     7	    cur.execute(f"PRAGMA table_info({table})")
     8	    return any(row[1] == column for row in cur.fetchall())
     9	
    10	
    11	def ensure_auth_schema() -> None:
    12	    """Create users table if missing, add soft-delete columns to sales,
    13	    create the covering index, seed the default owner on a fresh DB."""
    14	    conn = get_connection()
    15	    try:
    16	        cur = conn.cursor()
    17	        cur.execute("""
    18	            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS users (
    19	                id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    20	                username TEXT UNIQUE NOT NULL,
    21	                password_hash TEXT NOT NULL,
    22	                full_name TEXT,
    23	                role TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'cashier',
    24	                is_active INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
    25	                created_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
    26	            )
    27	        """)
    28	        for col, ddl in (
    29	            ("deleted_at",    "TEXT DEFAULT NULL"),
    30	            ("deleted_by",    "INTEGER DEFAULT NULL"),
    31	            ("delete_reason", "TEXT DEFAULT NULL"),
    32	        ):
    33	            if not _column_exists(cur, "sales", col):
    34	                cur.execute(f"ALTER TABLE sales ADD COLUMN {col} {ddl}")
    35	
    36	        cur.execute("""
    37	            CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_sales_date_active
    38	            ON sales(date, deleted_at)
    39	        """)
    40	        conn.commit()
    41	
    42	        cur.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM users")
    43	        if cur.fetchone()[0] == 0:
    44	            _auth._create_user(conn, "owner", "owner123", "owner", "Owner")
    45	            conn.commit()
    46	    finally:
    47	        conn.close()

     1	"""Product queries and mutations."""
     2	from .connection import get_connection
     3	from .errors import BarcodeExists
     4	
     5	__all__ = [
     6	    "get_all_products", "get_telecom_products", "get_product_by_barcode",
     7	    "search_products_by_name", "add_product", "update_product",
     8	    "delete_product", "update_stock",
     9	]
    10	
    11	
    12	_PRODUCT_COLUMNS = (
    13	    "id, barcode, name, photo, buy_price, sell_price, unit, stock, "
    14	    "single_price, pieces_per_pack, product_type, single_cigarette_price"
    15	)
    16	
    17	
    18	def get_all_products():
    19	    conn = get_connection()
    20	    try:
    21	        cur = conn.cursor()
    22	        cur.execute(f"SELECT {_PRODUCT_COLUMNS} FROM products ORDER BY name")
    23	        return cur.fetchall()
    24	    finally:
    25	        conn.close()
    26	
    27	
    28	def get_telecom_products():
    29	    conn = get_connection()
    30	    try:
    31	        cur = conn.cursor()
    32	        cur.execute(
    33	            "SELECT id, name, buy_price, sell_price, product_type "
    34	            "FROM products WHERE product_type = 'telecom' ORDER BY name"
    35	        )
    36	        return cur.fetchall()
    37	    finally:
    38	        conn.close()
    39	
    40	
    41	def get_product_by_barcode(barcode):
    42	    conn = get_connection()
    43	    try:
    44	        cur = conn.cursor()
    45	        cur.execute("SELECT * FROM products WHERE barcode = ?", (barcode,))
    46	        return cur.fetchone()
    47	    finally:
    48	        conn.close()
    49	
    50	
    51	def search_products_by_name(search_term):
    52	    conn = get_connection()
    53	    try:
    54	        cur = conn.cursor()
    55	        cur.execute(
    56	            f"SELECT {_PRODUCT_COLUMNS} FROM products "
    57	            "WHERE name LIKE ? ORDER BY name",
    58	            (f"%{search_term}%",),
    59	        )
    60	        return cur.fetchall()
    61	    finally:
    62	        conn.close()
    63	
    64	
    65	def _assert_barcode_free(cur, barcode, exclude_id=None):
    66	    if not barcode or not barcode.strip():
    67	        return
    68	    if exclude_id is None:
    69	        cur.execute("SELECT id FROM products WHERE barcode = ?", (barcode,))
    70	    else:
    71	        cur.execute(
    72	            "SELECT id FROM products WHERE barcode = ? AND id != ?",
    73	            (barcode, exclude_id),
    74	        )
    75	    if cur.fetchone():
    76	        raise BarcodeExists(barcode)
    77	
    78	
    79	def add_product(barcode, name, photo, buy_price, sell_price, unit, stock,
    80	                single_price=0, pieces_per_pack=20, product_type="normal",
    81	                single_cigarette_price=0):
    82	    conn = get_connection()
    83	    try:
    84	        cur = conn.cursor()
    85	        _assert_barcode_free(cur, barcode)
    86	        cur.execute("""
    87	            INSERT INTO products
    88	                (barcode, name, photo, buy_price, sell_price, unit, stock,
    89	                 single_price, pieces_per_pack, product_type, single_cigarette_price)
    90	            VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
    91	        """, (barcode, name, photo, buy_price, sell_price, unit, stock,
    92	              single_price, pieces_per_pack, product_type, single_cigarette_price))
    93	        conn.commit()
    94	    except Exception:
    95	        conn.rollback()
    96	        raise
    97	    finally:
    98	        conn.close()
    99	
   100	
   101	def update_product(id, barcode, name, photo, buy_price, sell_price, unit, stock,
   102	                   single_price=0, pieces_per_pack=20, product_type="normal",
   103	                   single_cigarette_price=0):
   104	    conn = get_connection()
   105	    try:
   106	        cur = conn.cursor()
   107	        _assert_barcode_free(cur, barcode, exclude_id=id)
   108	        cur.execute("""
   109	            UPDATE products
   110	            SET barcode = ?, name = ?, photo = ?, buy_price = ?, sell_price = ?,
   111	                unit = ?, stock = ?, single_price = ?, pieces_per_pack = ?,
   112	                product_type = ?, single_cigarette_price = ?
   113	            WHERE id = ?
   114	        """, (barcode, name, photo, buy_price, sell_price, unit, stock,
   115	              single_price, pieces_per_pack, product_type,
   116	              single_cigarette_price, id))
   117	        conn.commit()
   118	    except Exception:
   119	        conn.rollback()
   120	        raise
   121	    finally:
   122	        conn.close()
   123	
   124	
   125	def delete_product(id):
   126	    conn = get_connection()
   127	    try:
   128	        cur = conn.cursor()
   129	        cur.execute("DELETE FROM products WHERE id = ?", (id,))
   130	        conn.commit()
   131	    except Exception:
   132	        conn.rollback()
   133	        raise
   134	    finally:
   135	        conn.close()
   136	
   137	
   138	def update_stock(product_id, quantity_sold):
   139	    conn = get_connection()
   140	    try:
   141	        cur = conn.cursor()
   142	        cur.execute("UPDATE products SET stock = stock - ? WHERE id = ?",
   143	                    (quantity_sold, product_id))
   144	        conn.commit()
   145	    except Exception:
   146	        conn.rollback()
   147	        raise
   148	    finally:
   149	        conn.close()
=================================================================
FILE: database/reports.py

     1	"""Aggregates and reporting queries."""
     2	from .connection import get_connection
     3	
     4	__all__ = [
     5	    "get_period_summary_datetime", "get_top_products_datetime",
     6	    "get_stock_status", "verify_sales_aggregates",
     7	]
     8	
     9	
    10	def get_period_summary_datetime(start_dt, end_dt):
    11	    conn = get_connection()
    12	    try:
    13	        cur = conn.cursor()
    14	        cur.execute("""
    15	            SELECT COALESCE(SUM(total), 0),
    16	                   COALESCE(SUM(profit), 0),
    17	                   COUNT(DISTINCT date)
    18	            FROM sales
    19	            WHERE date BETWEEN ? AND ? AND deleted_at IS NULL
    20	        """, (start_dt, end_dt))
    21	        return cur.fetchone()
    22	    finally:
    23	        conn.close()
    24	
    25	
    26	def get_top_products_datetime(limit=10, start_dt=None, end_dt=None):
    27	    conn = get_connection()
    28	    try:
    29	        cur = conn.cursor()
    30	        query = """
    31	            SELECT p.name, SUM(s.quantity), SUM(s.total), SUM(s.profit)
    32	            FROM sales s JOIN products p ON s.product_id = p.id
    33	            WHERE s.deleted_at IS NULL
    34	        """
    35	        params = []
    36	        if start_dt and end_dt:
    37	            query += " AND s.date BETWEEN ? AND ?"
    38	            params.extend([start_dt, end_dt])
    39	        query += " GROUP BY p.id ORDER BY SUM(s.total) DESC LIMIT ?"
    40	        params.append(limit)
    41	        cur.execute(query, params)
    42	        return cur.fetchall()
    43	    finally:
    44	        conn.close()
    45	
    46	
    47	def get_stock_status(threshold=10):
    48	    conn = get_connection()
    49	    try:
    50	        cur = conn.cursor()
    51	        cur.execute("SELECT COALESCE(SUM(buy_price * stock), 0) FROM products")
    52	        total_value = cur.fetchone()[0]
    53	        cur.execute("""
    54	            SELECT name, stock, unit FROM products
    55	            WHERE stock < ? ORDER BY stock ASC
    56	        """, (threshold,))
    57	        low_stock = cur.fetchall()
    58	        return total_value, low_stock
    59	    finally:
    60	        conn.close()
    61	
    62	
    63	def verify_sales_aggregates():
    64	    conn = get_connection()
    65	    try:
    66	        cur = conn.cursor()
    67	        cur.execute("SELECT COALESCE(SUM(total), 0), COALESCE(SUM(profit), 0) "
    68	                    "FROM sales WHERE deleted_at IS NULL")
    69	        all_total, all_profit = cur.fetchone()
    70	        cur.execute("SELECT date, SUM(total), SUM(profit) "
    71	                    "FROM sales WHERE deleted_at IS NULL GROUP BY date")
    72	        groups = cur.fetchall()
    73	        grouped_total = sum(g[1] for g in groups)
    74	        grouped_profit = sum(g[2] for g in groups)
    75	    finally:
    76	        conn.close()
    77	
    78	    print(f"All sales:   total={all_total:.2f}, profit={all_profit:.2f}")
    79	    print(f"Grouped sum: total={grouped_total:.2f}, profit={grouped_profit:.2f}")
    80	    if abs(all_total - grouped_total) > 0.01:
    81	        print("MISMATCH DETECTED!")
    82	    return all_total, all_profit, grouped_total, grouped_profit

     1	"""Sales operations: checkout, soft/hard delete, trash, restore."""
     2	from datetime import datetime
     3	
     4	from .connection import get_connection
     5	from .errors import (
     6	    SaleNotFound, SaleAlreadyDeleted, SaleNotDeleted,
     7	    NoActiveSales, NoSales, NoDeletedSales, InsufficientStock,
     8	)
     9	
    10	__all__ = [
    11	    "add_sale", "delete_sale", "soft_delete_sale",
    12	    "soft_delete_sales_by_timestamp", "hard_delete_sales_by_timestamp",
    13	    "get_grouped_sales", "get_sales_by_timestamp",
    14	    "get_deleted_groups", "get_deleted_sales_by_deletion_timestamp",
    15	    "restore_sale", "restore_sales_by_deletion_timestamp",
    16	]
    17	
    18	
    19	def _now() -> str:
    20	    return datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")
    21	
    22	
    23	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    24	# Create
    25	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    26	def add_sale(product_id, quantity, profit, total, date=None):
    27	    conn = get_connection()
    28	    try:
    29	        cur = conn.cursor()
    30	        date = date or _now()
    31	        cur.execute(
    32	            "INSERT INTO sales (product_id, quantity, profit, total, date) "
    33	            "VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)",
    34	            (product_id, quantity, profit, total, date),
    35	        )
    36	        sale_id = cur.lastrowid
    37	        cur.execute("UPDATE products SET stock = stock - ? WHERE id = ?",
    38	                    (quantity, product_id))
    39	        conn.commit()
    40	        return sale_id
    41	    except Exception:
    42	        conn.rollback()
    43	        raise
    44	    finally:
    45	        conn.close()
    46	
    47	
    48	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    49	# Delete — single line
    50	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    51	def delete_sale(sale_id):
    52	    """HARD delete of one line. Owner only at the UI layer."""
    53	    conn = get_connection()
    54	    try:
    55	        cur = conn.cursor()
    56	        cur.execute("SELECT product_id, quantity, deleted_at FROM sales WHERE id = ?",
    57	                    (sale_id,))
    58	        row = cur.fetchone()
    59	        if not row:
    60	            raise SaleNotFound()
    61	        product_id, quantity, deleted_at = row
    62	        if deleted_at is None:
    63	            cur.execute("UPDATE products SET stock = stock + ? WHERE id = ?",
    64	                        (quantity, product_id))
    65	        cur.execute("DELETE FROM sales WHERE id = ?", (sale_id,))
    66	        conn.commit()
    67	        return True
    68	    except Exception:
    69	        conn.rollback()
    70	        raise
    71	    finally:
    72	        conn.close()
    73	
    74	
    75	def soft_delete_sale(sale_id, user_id, reason=None):
    76	    conn = get_connection()
    77	    try:
    78	        cur = conn.cursor()
    79	        cur.execute("SELECT product_id, quantity, deleted_at FROM sales WHERE id = ?",
    80	                    (sale_id,))
    81	        row = cur.fetchone()
    82	        if not row:
    83	            raise SaleNotFound()
    84	        product_id, quantity, deleted_at = row
    85	        if deleted_at is not None:
    86	            return True   # already deleted → idempotent
    87	
    88	        cur.execute("UPDATE products SET stock = stock + ? WHERE id = ?",
    89	                    (quantity, product_id))
    90	        cur.execute("""
    91	            UPDATE sales
    92	            SET deleted_at = ?, deleted_by = ?, delete_reason = ?
    93	            WHERE id = ?
    94	        """, (_now(), user_id, reason, sale_id))
    95	        conn.commit()
    96	        return True
    97	    except Exception:
    98	        conn.rollback()
    99	        raise
   100	    finally:
   101	        conn.close()
   102	
   103	
   104	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   105	# Delete — by cart timestamp
   106	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   107	def soft_delete_sales_by_timestamp(timestamp, user_id, reason=None):
   108	    conn = get_connection()
   109	    try:
   110	        cur = conn.cursor()
   111	        cur.execute("""
   112	            SELECT id, product_id, quantity FROM sales
   113	            WHERE date = ? AND deleted_at IS NULL
   114	        """, (timestamp,))
   115	        rows = cur.fetchall()
   116	        if not rows:
   117	            raise NoActiveSales()
   118	
   119	        now = _now()
   120	        for sale_id, product_id, quantity in rows:
   121	            cur.execute("UPDATE products SET stock = stock + ? WHERE id = ?",
   122	                        (quantity, product_id))
   123	            cur.execute("""
   124	                UPDATE sales
   125	                SET deleted_at = ?, deleted_by = ?, delete_reason = ?
   126	                WHERE id = ?
   127	            """, (now, user_id, reason, sale_id))
   128	        conn.commit()
   129	        return len(rows)
   130	    except Exception:
   131	        conn.rollback()
   132	        raise
   133	    finally:
   134	        conn.close()
   135	
   136	
   137	def hard_delete_sales_by_timestamp(timestamp):
   138	    conn = get_connection()
   139	    try:
   140	        cur = conn.cursor()
   141	        cur.execute("""
   142	            SELECT id, product_id, quantity, deleted_at FROM sales
   143	            WHERE date = ?
   144	        """, (timestamp,))
   145	        rows = cur.fetchall()
   146	        if not rows:
   147	            raise NoSales()
   148	
   149	        for sale_id, product_id, quantity, deleted_at in rows:
   150	            if deleted_at is None:
   151	                cur.execute("UPDATE products SET stock = stock + ? WHERE id = ?",
   152	                            (quantity, product_id))
   153	            cur.execute("DELETE FROM sales WHERE id = ?", (sale_id,))
   154	        conn.commit()
   155	        return len(rows)
   156	    except Exception:
   157	        conn.rollback()
   158	        raise
   159	    finally:
   160	        conn.close()
   161	
   162	
   163	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   164	# Read — active
   165	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   166	def get_grouped_sales(start_datetime=None, end_datetime=None):
   167	    conn = get_connection()
   168	    try:
   169	        cur = conn.cursor()
   170	        query = ("SELECT date, COUNT(*), SUM(total), SUM(profit) "
   171	                 "FROM sales WHERE deleted_at IS NULL")
   172	        params = []
   173	        if start_datetime:
   174	            query += " AND date >= ?"
   175	            params.append(start_datetime)
   176	        if end_datetime:
   177	            query += " AND date <= ?"
   178	            params.append(end_datetime)
   179	        query += " GROUP BY date ORDER BY date DESC"
   180	        cur.execute(query, params)
   181	        return cur.fetchall()
   182	    finally:
   183	        conn.close()
   184	
   185	
   186	def get_sales_by_timestamp(timestamp):
   187	    conn = get_connection()
   188	    try:
   189	        cur = conn.cursor()
   190	        cur.execute("""
   191	            SELECT s.id, p.name, s.quantity, p.unit, s.total, s.profit
   192	            FROM sales s JOIN products p ON s.product_id = p.id
   193	            WHERE s.date = ? AND s.deleted_at IS NULL
   194	        """, (timestamp,))
   195	        return cur.fetchall()
   196	    finally:
   197	        conn.close()
   198	
   199	
   200	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   201	# Read — trash
   202	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   203	def get_deleted_groups(start_dt=None, end_dt=None):
   204	    conn = get_connection()
   205	    try:
   206	        cur = conn.cursor()
   207	        query = """
   208	            SELECT s.deleted_at, COUNT(*), SUM(s.total), SUM(s.profit),
   209	                   COALESCE(MAX(u.username), '(inconnu)')
   210	            FROM sales s
   211	            LEFT JOIN users u ON s.deleted_by = u.id
   212	            WHERE s.deleted_at IS NOT NULL
   213	        """
   214	        params = []
   215	        if start_dt:
   216	            query += " AND s.deleted_at >= ?"
   217	            params.append(start_dt)
   218	        if end_dt:
   219	            query += " AND s.deleted_at <= ?"
   220	            params.append(end_dt)
   221	        query += " GROUP BY s.deleted_at ORDER BY s.deleted_at DESC"
   222	        cur.execute(query, params)
   223	        return cur.fetchall()
   224	    finally:
   225	        conn.close()
   226	
   227	
   228	def get_deleted_sales_by_deletion_timestamp(deleted_at):
   229	    conn = get_connection()
   230	    try:
   231	        cur = conn.cursor()
   232	        cur.execute("""
   233	            SELECT s.id, s.date, p.name, s.quantity, p.unit, s.total, s.profit,
   234	                   COALESCE(u.username, '(inconnu)'), s.delete_reason
   235	            FROM sales s
   236	            JOIN products p ON s.product_id = p.id
   237	            LEFT JOIN users u ON s.deleted_by = u.id
   238	            WHERE s.deleted_at = ?
   239	            ORDER BY s.id
   240	        """, (deleted_at,))
   241	        return cur.fetchall()
   242	    finally:
   243	        conn.close()
   244	
   245	
   246	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   247	# Restore
   248	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   249	def _assert_restorable(cur, rows):
   250	    """All-or-nothing stock check before restoring a batch."""
   251	    for _sale_id, product_id, quantity in rows:
   252	        cur.execute("SELECT stock FROM products WHERE id = ?", (product_id,))
   253	        r = cur.fetchone()
   254	        if not r:
   255	            raise SaleNotFound()  # product gone
   256	        if r[0] < quantity:
   257	            raise InsufficientStock(available=r[0], required=quantity)
   258	
   259	
   260	def restore_sale(sale_id):
   261	    conn = get_connection()
   262	    try:
   263	        cur = conn.cursor()
   264	        cur.execute("SELECT product_id, quantity, deleted_at FROM sales WHERE id = ?",
   265	                    (sale_id,))
   266	        row = cur.fetchone()
   267	        if not row:
   268	            raise SaleNotFound()
   269	        product_id, quantity, deleted_at = row
   270	        if deleted_at is None:
   271	            raise SaleNotDeleted()
   272	
   273	        cur.execute("SELECT stock FROM products WHERE id = ?", (product_id,))
   274	        r = cur.fetchone()
   275	        if not r:
   276	            raise SaleNotFound()
   277	        if r[0] < quantity:
   278	            raise InsufficientStock(available=r[0], required=quantity)
   279	
   280	        cur.execute("UPDATE products SET stock = stock - ? WHERE id = ?",
   281	                    (quantity, product_id))
   282	        cur.execute("""
   283	            UPDATE sales
   284	            SET deleted_at = NULL, deleted_by = NULL, delete_reason = NULL
   285	            WHERE id = ?
   286	        """, (sale_id,))
   287	        conn.commit()
   288	        return True
   289	    except Exception:
   290	        conn.rollback()
   291	        raise
   292	    finally:
   293	        conn.close()
   294	
   295	
   296	def restore_sales_by_deletion_timestamp(deleted_at):
   297	    conn = get_connection()
   298	    try:
   299	        cur = conn.cursor()
   300	        cur.execute("SELECT id, product_id, quantity FROM sales WHERE deleted_at = ?",
   301	                    (deleted_at,))
   302	        rows = cur.fetchall()
   303	        if not rows:
   304	            raise NoDeletedSales()
   305	
   306	        _assert_restorable(cur, rows)
   307	
   308	        for sale_id, product_id, quantity in rows:
   309	            cur.execute("UPDATE products SET stock = stock - ? WHERE id = ?",
   310	                        (quantity, product_id))
   311	            cur.execute("""
   312	                UPDATE sales
   313	                SET deleted_at = NULL, deleted_by = NULL, delete_reason = NULL
   314	                WHERE id = ?
   315	            """, (sale_id,))
   316	        conn.commit()
   317	        return len(rows)
   318	    except Exception:
   319	        conn.rollback()
   320	        raise
   321	    finally:
   322	        conn.close()

     1	"""Schema creation. Only called on first run or when the DB is empty."""
     2	import sqlite3
     3	
     4	from .connection import DB_PATH
     5	
     6	
     7	def create_tables() -> None:
     8	    """Create products + sales tables and seed telecom products."""
     9	    conn = sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH)
    10	    cursor = conn.cursor()
    11	
    12	    # Fresh-install path only — main.py guards this.
    13	    cursor.execute("DROP TABLE IF EXISTS products")
    14	    cursor.execute("DROP TABLE IF EXISTS sales")
    15	
    16	    cursor.execute("""
    17	        CREATE TABLE products (
    18	            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    19	            barcode TEXT,
    20	            name TEXT NOT NULL,
    21	            photo TEXT,
    22	            buy_price REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    23	            sell_price REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    24	            unit TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'piece',
    25	            stock REAL DEFAULT 0,
    26	            single_price REAL DEFAULT 0,
    27	            pieces_per_pack INTEGER DEFAULT 20,
    28	            product_type TEXT DEFAULT 'normal',
    29	            single_cigarette_price REAL DEFAULT 0
    30	        )
    31	    """)
    32	
    33	    cursor.execute("""
    34	        CREATE TABLE sales (
    35	            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    36	            product_id INTEGER,
    37	            quantity REAL NOT NULL,
    38	            profit REAL NOT NULL,
    39	            total REAL NOT NULL,
    40	            date TEXT NOT NULL,
    41	            FOREIGN KEY (product_id) REFERENCES products (id)
    42	        )
    43	    """)
    44	
    45	    telecom = [
    46	        ("TELECOM 1.3 DT", "telecom_1.3", 0.8, 1.3, "piece", 0, "telecom"),
    47	        ("TELECOM 5.9 DT", "telecom_5.9", 4.5, 5.9, "piece", 0, "telecom"),
    48	        ("ORANGE 1.3 DT",  "orange_1.3",  0.8, 1.3, "piece", 0, "telecom"),
    49	        ("ORANGE 5.9 DT",  "orange_5.9",  4.5, 5.9, "piece", 0, "telecom"),
    50	        ("OOREDOO 1.3 DT", "ooredoo_1.3", 0.8, 1.3, "piece", 0, "telecom"),
    51	        ("OOREDOO 5.9 DT", "ooredoo_5.9", 4.5, 5.9, "piece", 0, "telecom"),
    52	    ]
    53	    for name, barcode, buy, sell, unit, stock, ptype in telecom:
    54	        cursor.execute(
    55	            "INSERT INTO products "
    56	            "(name, barcode, buy_price, sell_price, unit, stock, product_type) "
    57	            "VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)",
    58	            (name, barcode, buy, sell, unit, stock, ptype),
    59	        )
    60	
    61	    conn.commit()
    62	    conn.close()

     1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
     2	"""Stock Manager — entry point."""
     3	import os
     4	import locale
     5	
     6	# Must run BEFORE tkinter is imported, so Arabic shaping and bidi work.
     7	os.environ.setdefault("LC_ALL", "C.UTF-8")
     8	os.environ.setdefault("LANG", "C.UTF-8")
     9	try:
    10	    locale.setlocale(locale.LC_ALL, "")
    11	except locale.Error:
    12	    pass
    13	
    14	from app.router import StockManager
    15	
    16	
    17	def main() -> None:
    18	    app = StockManager()
    19	    if app.user:
    20	        app.run()
    21	
    22	
    23	if __name__ == "__main__":
    24	    main()

FILE: project_dump.sh
=================================================================

     1	#!/bin/bash
     2	
     3	# Script to display Python project structure and file contents
     4	# Usage: ./project_dump.sh [directory_path] [output_file]
     5	
     6	# Set the target directory (default to current directory)
     7	TARGET_DIR="${1:-.}"
     8	OUTPUT_FILE="${2:-project_dump.txt}"
     9	
    10	# Check if the directory exists
    11	if [ ! -d "$TARGET_DIR" ]; then
    12	    echo "Error: Directory '$TARGET_DIR' does not exist."
    13	    exit 1
    14	fi
    15	
    16	# Create or clear the output file
    17	> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
    18	
    19	# Function to print separator lines
    20	print_separator() {
    21	    echo "=================================================================" >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
    22	    echo "$1" >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
    23	    echo "=================================================================" >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
    24	    echo "" >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
    25	}
    26	
    27	# Function to check if file should be included
    28	should_include_file() {
    29	    local file="$1"
    30	    
    31	    # Exclude common Python cache and database files
    32	    case "$file" in
    33	        *.pyc|*.pyo|*.pyd) return 1 ;;
    34	        __pycache__/*) return 1 ;;
    35	        *.db|*.sqlite|*.sqlite3) return 1 ;;
    36	        *.log|*.tmp|*.temp) return 1 ;;
    37	        *.egg-info/*) return 1 ;;
    38	        *.egg) return 1 ;;
    39	        *.so|*.dll|*.dylib) return 1 ;;
    40	        *.bak|*.swp|*.swo) return 1 ;;
    41	        .git/*) return 1 ;;
    42	        .venv/*|venv/*|env/*|.env/*) return 1 ;;
    43	        .pytest_cache/*) return 1 ;;
    44	        .mypy_cache/*) return 1 ;;
    45	        .coverage) return 1 ;;
    46	        .DS_Store) return 1 ;;
    47	        "$OUTPUT_FILE") return 1 ;;  # Exclude the output file itself
    48	        *)
    49	            # Only include text files (common Python extensions and config files)
    50	            case "$file" in
    51	                *.py|*.txt|*.md|*.json|*.yml|*.yaml|*.ini|*.cfg|\
    52	                *.toml|*.rst|*.html|*.css|*.js|*.sh|*.env.example|\
    53	                .gitignore|.dockerignore|Dockerfile|Makefile|\
    54	                requirements*.txt|pyproject.toml|setup.py|setup.cfg|\
    55	                README*|LICENSE*|CHANGELOG*)
    56	                    return 0 ;;
    57	                *)
    58	                    # Check if it's a text file using file command
    59	                    if file "$file" | grep -q "text"; then
    60	                        return 0
    61	                    fi
    62	                    return 1 ;;
    63	            esac ;;
    64	    esac
    65	}
    66	
    67	print_separator "PROJECT STRUCTURE"
    68	echo "Project directory: $(realpath "$TARGET_DIR")" >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
    69	echo "" >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
    70	
    71	# Generate tree structure (if tree command is available)
    72	if command -v tree &> /dev/null; then
    73	    echo "Directory structure (using tree):" >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
    74	    tree -I '__pycache__|*.pyc|*.db|*.sqlite|*.sqlite3|*.log|.git|.venv|venv|env|.env|*.egg-info|.pytest_cache|.mypy_cache' "$TARGET_DIR" >> "$OUTPUT_FILE" 2>/dev/null
    75	else
    76	    echo "Note: Install 'tree' command for better directory visualization." >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
    77	    echo "Basic directory listing:" >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
    78	    find "$TARGET_DIR" -type d -not -path "*/__pycache__/*" -not -path "*/.git/*" \
    79	        -not -path "*/.venv/*" -not -path "*/venv/*" -not -path "*/env/*" \
    80	        -not -path "*/.pytest_cache/*" -not -path "*/.mypy_cache/*" | sort >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
    81	fi
    82	
    83	echo "" >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
    84	print_separator "FILE CONTENTS"
    85	
    86	# Find and display contents of all text files
    87	find "$TARGET_DIR" -type f -not -path "*/__pycache__/*" -not -path "*/.git/*" \
    88	    -not -path "*/.venv/*" -not -path "*/venv/*" -not -path "*/env/*" \
    89	    -not -path "*/.pytest_cache/*" -not -path "*/.mypy_cache/*" \
    90	    -not -name "*.pyc" -not -name "*.pyo" -not -name "*.pyd" \
    91	    -not -name "*.db" -not -name "*.sqlite" -not -name "*.sqlite3" \
    92	    -not -name "*.log" -not -name "*.egg" \
    93	    -not -path "*.egg-info/*" | sort | while read -r file; do
    94	    
    95	    # Double-check if file should be included
    96	    if should_include_file "$file"; then
    97	        # Get relative path
    98	        rel_path="${file#$TARGET_DIR/}"
    99	        if [ "$rel_path" = "$file" ]; then
   100	            rel_path="$file"
   101	        fi
   102	        
   103	        print_separator "FILE: $rel_path"
   104	        
   105	        # Try to display file with line numbers
   106	        if [ -r "$file" ]; then
   107	            # Check if file is empty
   108	            if [ -s "$file" ]; then
   109	                # Use cat -n for line numbers
   110	                cat -n "$file" >> "$OUTPUT_FILE" 2>/dev/null || echo "Error reading file" >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
   111	            else
   112	                echo "(empty file)" >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
   113	            fi
   114	        else
   115	            echo "(file not readable)" >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
   116	        fi
   117	        echo "" >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
   118	    fi
   119	done
   120	
   121	echo "Project dump complete! Output saved to: $OUTPUT_FILE"
   122	echo "Total size: $(du -h "$OUTPUT_FILE" | cut -f1)"
   123	
   124	# Optional: Show summary
   125	echo ""
   126	echo "Summary of processed files:"
   127	find "$TARGET_DIR" -type f -not -path "*/__pycache__/*" -not -path "*/.git/*" \
   128	    -not -path "*/.venv/*" -not -path "*/venv/*" -not -path "*/env/*" \
   129	    -not -path "*/.pytest_cache/*" -not -path "*/.mypy_cache/*" \
   130	    -not -name "*.pyc" -not -name "*.pyo" -not -name "*.pyd" \
   131	    -not -name "*.db" -not -name "*.sqlite" -not -name "*.sqlite3" \
   132	    -not -name "*.log" -not -name "*.egg" \
   133	    -not -path "*.egg-info/*" | wc -l | xargs echo "Total files included:"

FILE: README.md

     1	# 📦 Stock Manager
     2	
     3	> A tiny point-of-sale and inventory app for small shops.
     4	> Scan it. Sell it. Count it. Go home on time.
     5	
     6	![Python](https://img.shields.io/badge/python-3.9%2B-blue)
     7	![UI](https://img.shields.io/badge/UI-Tkinter-orange)
     8	![DB](https://img.shields.io/badge/database-SQLite-lightgrey)
     9	![i18n](https://img.shields.io/badge/languages-EN%20%7C%20FR%20%7C%20AR-green)
    10	![License](https://img.shields.io/badge/license-MIT-brightgreen)
    11	![Vibes](https://img.shields.io/badge/vibecoded-100%25-ff69b4)
    12	
    13	---
    14	
    15	## 🎯 What is this?
    16	
    17	**Stock Manager** is a desktop app that runs a small shop's counter: ring up sales with a barcode scanner (or the keyboard), keep stock honest, see how the day went, and undo mistakes without panic.
    18	
    19	It runs on the Python standard library. No server, no cloud, no subscription. Just a single SQLite file and a window.
    20	
    21	## 🤖 The honest part: it's vibecoded
    22	
    23	This project was **vibecoded**: built in conversation with AI, iterated on feel and feedback, and shaped by what a real shop counter needs rather than by a grand architecture document.
    24	
    25	**Why does it exist?** To earn a little money on a small scale, and that money is **feeding a bigger project** I'm building on the side. Think of Stock Manager as the scrappy little sibling that pays the rent while the big one grows up.
    26	
    27	What that means for you:
    28	
    29	- ✅ It works, and it's used for real sales.
    30	- ✅ The code is readable, commented where it matters, and kept tidy.
    31	- ⚠️ It hasn't been through a formal audit or a test suite. Treat it like a good prototype that went to work, and **back up your data** (the app helps, see below).
    32	- 🙏 Bug reports and PRs are very welcome.
    33	
    34	## ✨ Features
    35	
    36	| | |
    37	|---|---|
    38	| 🛒 **Fast sales screen** | Barcode scan (digits) or name search (letters). Space bar to pay. Built for speed at the counter. |
    39	| 📱 **Telecom top-ups** | One-key shortcuts **F1–F6** for Telecom / Orange / Ooredoo credit (1.3 DT and 5.9 DT). |
    40	| ⚖️ **Flexible units** | Sell by piece, by weight (g / kg), by pack, or cigarettes by single stick or full pack. |
    41	| 🗂️ **Product management** | Add, edit, search, restock. Optional product photos. |
    42	| 🧾 **Sales history** | Grouped by cart, filterable by date and time, with per-item details. |
    43	| 🗑️ **Trash (corbeille)** | Soft-deleted sales can be restored with stock re-adjusted. Owners can also delete permanently. |
    44	| 📊 **Reports** | Period summary, top products, low-stock alerts, and exportable text reports. "Since last report" in one click. |
    45	| 👥 **Users & roles** | **Owner** (everything) and **Cashier** (sell, view history and reports, soft delete). PBKDF2-hashed passwords. |
    46	| 💾 **Automatic backups** | Hourly, on exit, and after each sale. The app keeps the last 10 automatic backups. |
    47	| 🌍 **Three languages** | English, Français, العربية, with proper **RTL layout** and Arabic text shaping. |
    48	
    49	## 🚀 Quick start
    50	
    51	```bash
    52	git clone https://github.com/<your-username>/stock-manager.git
    53	cd stock-manager
    54	python -m venv .venv && source .venv/bin/activate   # Windows: .venv\Scripts\activate
    55	pip install -r requirements.txt
    56	python main.py
    57	```
    58	
    59	The database (`stock.db`) is created automatically on first launch, along with the default telecom products.
    60	
    61	> **Requirements:** Python 3.9+ with Tkinter. On some Linux distros you may need `sudo dnf install python3-tkinter` (Fedora) or `sudo apt install python3-tk` (Debian/Ubuntu).
    62	
    63	### 🔑 First login
    64	
    65	| Username | Password |
    66	|---|---|
    67	| `owner` | `owner123` |
    68	
    69	**Change this password immediately** from the Users screen. Seriously. Right now. We'll wait.
    70	
    71	## ⌨️ Keyboard shortcuts (Sales screen)
    72	
    73	| Key | Action |
    74	|---|---|
    75	| `F1` – `F6` | Add Telecom / Orange / Ooredoo credit to cart |
    76	| `Space` | Pay (when the cart is not empty and you're not typing) |
    77	| `↓` | Jump into search results |
    78	| `Enter` | Select result / confirm popup |
    79	| `U` | Switch unit in a quantity popup |
    80	| `Esc` | Cancel popup or search |
    81	
    82	## 🌐 Languages & Arabic support
    83	
    84	Switch language on the login screen. The choice is saved in `settings.json`.
    85	
    86	For correct Arabic letter shaping and right-to-left ordering in Tk, install the optional libraries (already listed in `requirements.txt`):
    87	
    88	```bash
    89	pip install arabic-reshaper python-bidi
    90	```
    91	
    92	Without them the app still runs, but Arabic text may look disconnected.
    93	
    94	## 🗺️ Project structure
    95	
    96	```
    97	stock_manager/
    98	├── main.py              # Entry point & screen switching
    99	├── database/
   100	│   ├── db_setup.py      # First-run table creation
   101	│   └── db_operations.py # All SQL lives here
   102	├── screens/             # One file per screen
   103	│   ├── sales.py
   104	│   ├── products.py
   105	│   ├── history.py
   106	│   ├── deleted_sales.py
   107	│   ├── reports.py
   108	│   ├── users.py
   109	│   └── login.py
   110	├── utils/
   111	│   ├── auth.py          # Users, roles, permissions, hashing
   112	│   ├── i18n.py          # Translations + RTL helpers
   113	│   ├── backup.py        # Hourly & on-exit backups
   114	│   ├── helpers.py
   115	│   └── last_report.py
   116	├── assets/products/     # Product photos
   117	├── backups/             # Auto-generated, git-ignored
   118	└── reports/             # Generated reports, git-ignored
   119	```
   120	
   121	## 🔒 Good to know
   122	
   123	- Your data (`stock.db`, `backups/`, `reports/`, `settings.json`) is **git-ignored on purpose**. Never commit a shop's sales.
   124	- Keep a copy of `backups/` somewhere other than the same machine (USB drive, cloud folder). A backup on the same disk won't help if the disk dies.
   125	- `create_tables()` in `db_setup.py` drops and recreates the `products` and `sales` tables. It only runs when the database is missing or has no `products` table, but be careful if you call it by hand.
   126	
   127	## 🛣️ Ideas for later
   128	
   129	- Receipt printing
   130	- CSV / Excel export
   131	- Stock-movement log (who changed what, and when)
   132	- Automated tests
   133	
   134	Got another idea? Open an issue.
   135	
   136	## 🤝 Contributing
   137	
   138	Contributions are welcome! Please read [CONTRIBUTING.md](CONTRIBUTING.md) first. The short version: open an issue for big changes, keep PRs focused, and don't break translations (every user-facing string goes through `t("key")` in all three languages).
   139	
   140	## 📄 License
   141	
   142	[MIT](LICENSE) © 2026 Saber Arbi

   147	  Built with ☕, vibes, and a bigger dream in the background.<br>
   148	  If this app helps your shop, that's the best kind of win. 🧡
   149	</p>

     1	# --- Required ---
     2	# (nothing strictly required — the app runs on the stdlib)

FILE: screens/base.py

     1	"""BaseScreen: shared chrome for every screen (topbar, frame, show/hide)."""
     2	import tkinter as tk
     3	
     4	from app import theme
     5	from utils import i18n
     6	
     7	
     8	class BaseScreen:
     9	    """Subclass and implement `build_body()` and (optionally) `build_topbar()`."""
    10	
    11	    topbar_color = theme.DARK
    12	
    13	    def __init__(self, root, switch_callback, user=None, logout=None):
    14	        self.root = root
    15	        self.switch = switch_callback
    16	        self.user = user
    17	        self.logout = logout              # ← new
    18	        self.frame = tk.Frame(root, bg=theme.BG)
    19	        self.build()
    20	
    21	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    22	    def build(self):
    23	        self.build_topbar()
    24	        self.build_body()
    25	
    26	    def build_topbar(self):
    27	        """Override to customise. Default: empty bar with title."""
    28	
    29	    def build_body(self):
    30	        raise NotImplementedError
    31	
    32	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    33	    def font(self, size: int, weight: str = "normal"):
    34	        return i18n.ui_font(size, weight)
    35	
    36	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    37	    def show(self):
    38	        self.frame.pack(fill="both", expand=True)
    39	        self.on_show()
    40	
    41	    def hide(self):
    42	        self.frame.pack_forget()
    43	        self.on_hide()
    44	
    45	    def on_show(self):
    46	        """Called after the screen becomes visible."""
    47	
    48	    def on_hide(self):
    49	        """Called before the screen is hidden."""

     1	"""Trash — restore or permanently delete soft-deleted sales."""
     2	import tkinter as tk
     3	from tkinter import messagebox, ttk
     4	
     5	from app import theme
     6	from database import db
     7	from database.errors import DomainError
     8	from screens.base import BaseScreen
     9	from screens.widgets import DateRangeFilter, ScrollableTreeview, build_topbar
    10	from utils import auth, i18n
    11	from utils.i18n import t
    12	
    13	
    14	class DeletedSalesScreen(BaseScreen):
    15	    topbar_color = theme.RED_DARKER
    16	
    17	    def __init__(self, root, switch, user=None, logout=None):
    18	        self._active_range = (None, None)
    19	        super().__init__(root, switch, user, logout=None)
    20	        self.reload()
    21	
    22	    def build_topbar(self):
    23	        start, end = [], []
    24	        start.append((t("nav.back_to_history"), theme.RED_DARK,
    25	                      lambda: self.switch("history")))
    26	        if auth.can(self.user, "view_reports"):
    27	            start.append((t("nav.reports_arrow"), theme.RED_DARK,
    28	                          lambda: self.switch("reports")))
    29	        if auth.can(self.user, "manage_users"):
    30	            end.append((t("nav.users_arrow"), theme.RED_DARK,
    31	                        lambda: self.switch("users")))
    32	        if auth.can(self.user, "sell"):
    33	            end.append((t("nav.sales_arrow"), theme.RED_DARK,
    34	                        lambda: self.switch("sales")))
    35	        build_topbar(self.frame, color=self.topbar_color,
    36	                     title_key="deleted.title",
    37	                     buttons_start=start, buttons_end=end,
    38	                     logout_callback=self.logout)
    39	
    40	    def build_body(self):
    41	        main = tk.Frame(self.frame, bg=theme.BG)
    42	        main.pack(fill="both", expand=True, padx=5, pady=5)
    43	
    44	        self.filter = DateRangeFilter(
    45	            main,
    46	            from_key="deleted.deleted_from", to_key="deleted.to",
    47	            on_apply=self.apply_filter,
    48	            on_clear=self.clear_filter,
    49	            on_refresh=self.reload,
    50	        )
    51	        self.filter.pack(fill="x", pady=(0, 5))
    52	
    53	        tk.Button(self.filter, text=t("deleted.view_all"), bg=theme.GRAY,
    54	                  fg="white", font=self.font(9), command=self.clear_filter
    55	                  ).pack(side=i18n.side_start(), padx=5)
    56	
    57	        summary = tk.Frame(self.filter, bg=theme.BG_CARD)
    58	        summary.pack(side=i18n.side_end(), padx=10, pady=6)
    59	        self.total_label = tk.Label(summary, text=f'{t("lbl.total")}: 0.00 DT',
    60	                                    bg=theme.BG_CARD, font=self.font(10, "bold"))
    61	        self.total_label.pack(side=i18n.side_start(), padx=5)
    62	        self.count_label = tk.Label(summary, text=f'{t("lbl.articles")}: 0',
    63	                                    bg=theme.BG_CARD, font=self.font(10, "bold"))
    64	        self.count_label.pack(side=i18n.side_start(), padx=5)
    65	
    66	        self.tree_widget = ScrollableTreeview(
    67	            main,
    68	            columns=("ddate", "dtime", "by", "count", "total", "profit"),
    69	            headings=[t("deleted.col_deleted_at"), t("deleted.col_time"),
    70	                      t("deleted.col_by"), t("deleted.col_items"),
    71	                      t("deleted.col_total"), t("deleted.col_profit")],
    72	            widths=[110, 70, 120, 80, 110, 110],
    73	        )
    74	        self.tree_widget.pack(fill="both", expand=True)
    75	        self.tree_widget.bind("<Double-1>", self.view_details)
    76	        self.tree = self.tree_widget.tree
    77	
    78	        self._build_footer(main)
    79	
    80	    def _build_footer(self, parent):
    81	        footer = tk.Frame(parent, bg=theme.BG)
    82	        footer.pack(fill="x", pady=5)
    83	
    84	        # Restore (anyone who can view deleted sales)
    85	        tk.Button(footer, text=t("deleted.restore_selection"),
    86	                  bg=theme.GREEN, fg="white",
    87	                  font=self.font(10), width=22,
    88	                  command=self.restore_selected
    89	                  ).pack(side=i18n.side_start(), padx=5)
    90	
    91	        # NEW: permanent delete (owner only — needs hard_delete_sale)
    92	        if auth.can(self.user, "hard_delete_sale"):
    93	            tk.Button(footer, text=t("deleted.hard_delete_selection"),
    94	                      bg=theme.RED, fg="white",
    95	                      font=self.font(10), width=24,
    96	                      command=self.hard_delete_selected
    97	                      ).pack(side=i18n.side_start(), padx=5)
    98	
    99	        tk.Button(footer, text=t("btn.details"), bg=theme.BLUE, fg="white",
   100	                  font=self.font(10), width=15,
   101	                  command=self.show_details_selected
   102	                  ).pack(side=i18n.side_start(), padx=5)
   103	
   104	        tk.Label(footer, text=t("deleted.restore_hint"),
   105	                 bg=theme.BG, fg=theme.ORANGE,
   106	                 font=self.font(9)).pack(side=i18n.side_end(), padx=5)
   107	
   108	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   109	    def reload(self):
   110	        self.tree_widget.clear()
   111	        start, end = self._active_range
   112	        groups = db.get_deleted_groups(start, end)
   113	        total_amount = 0.0
   114	        total_lines = 0
   115	        for deleted_at, count, amount, profit, username in groups:
   116	            date_part = deleted_at[:10] if deleted_at else ""
   117	            time_part = (deleted_at[11:16]
   118	                         if deleted_at and len(deleted_at) > 16 else "")
   119	            self.tree_widget.insert_row((
   120	                date_part, time_part, username, count,
   121	                f"{amount:.2f}", f"{profit:.2f}",
   122	            ), tags=(deleted_at,))
   123	            total_amount += amount or 0
   124	            total_lines += count or 0
   125	        self.total_label.config(text=f'{t("lbl.total")}: {total_amount:.2f} DT')
   126	        self.count_label.config(text=f'{t("lbl.articles")}: {total_lines}')
   127	
   128	    def apply_filter(self):
   129	        try:
   130	            self._active_range = self.filter.get_range()
   131	        except ValueError as e:
   132	            messagebox.showerror(t("msg.error"), str(e))
   133	            return
   134	        self.reload()
   135	
   136	    def clear_filter(self):
   137	        self._active_range = (None, None)
   138	        self.reload()
   139	
   140	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   141	    def restore_selected(self):
   142	        if not auth.can(self.user, "view_deleted_sales"):
   143	            messagebox.showerror(t("msg.access_denied"), t("msg.no_permission"))
   144	            return
   145	        stamps = self.tree_widget.selection_tags()
   146	        if not stamps:
   147	            messagebox.showwarning(t("msg.warning"),
   148	                                   t("deleted.select_to_restore"))
   149	            return
   150	        if not messagebox.askyesno(
   151	            t("msg.confirmation"),
   152	            t("deleted.confirm_restore", n=len(stamps)),
   153	        ):
   154	            return
   155	        errors = []
   156	        for ts in stamps:
   157	            try:
   158	                db.restore_sales_by_deletion_timestamp(ts)
   159	            except DomainError as e:
   160	                errors.append(f"{ts}: {t(e.key, **e.kwargs)}")
   161	            except Exception as e:
   162	                errors.append(f"{ts}: {e}")
   163	        self._report_bulk(errors, stamps, "deleted.restore_ok")
   164	
   165	    # NEW -----------------------------------------------------------------
   166	    def hard_delete_selected(self):
   167	        if not auth.can(self.user, "hard_delete_sale"):
   168	            messagebox.showerror(t("msg.access_denied"),
   169	                                 t("history.owner_only_hard"))
   170	            return
   171	        stamps = self.tree_widget.selection_tags()
   172	        if not stamps:
   173	            messagebox.showwarning(t("msg.warning"),
   174	                                   t("deleted.select_item"))
   175	            return
   176	        if not messagebox.askyesno(
   177	            t("msg.confirmation"),
   178	            t("deleted.confirm_hard", n=len(stamps)),
   179	        ):
   180	            return
   181	        errors = []
   182	        for ts in stamps:
   183	            try:
   184	                db.hard_delete_sales_by_timestamp(ts)
   185	            except DomainError as e:
   186	                errors.append(f"{ts}: {t(e.key, **e.kwargs)}")
   187	            except Exception as e:
   188	                errors.append(f"{ts}: {e}")
   189	        self._report_bulk(errors, stamps, "deleted.hard_deleted_ok")
   190	
   191	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   192	    def _report_bulk(self, errors, stamps, success_key):
   193	        if errors:
   194	            messagebox.showerror(t("msg.error"), "\n".join(errors[:5]))
   195	        else:
   196	            messagebox.showinfo(t("msg.success"),
   197	                                t(success_key, n=len(stamps)))
   198	        self.reload()
   199	
   200	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   201	    def show_details_selected(self):
   202	        stamps = self.tree_widget.selection_tags()
   203	        if not stamps:
   204	            messagebox.showwarning(t("msg.warning"), t("deleted.select_item"))
   205	            return
   206	        self.show_details_for_timestamp(stamps[0])
   207	
   208	    def view_details(self, event=None):
   209	        sel = self.tree.selection()
   210	        if sel:
   211	            ts = self.tree.item(sel[0], "tags")[0]
   212	            self.show_details_for_timestamp(ts)
   213	
   214	    def show_details_for_timestamp(self, deleted_at):
   215	        if not db.get_deleted_sales_by_deletion_timestamp(deleted_at):
   216	            messagebox.showinfo(t("msg.info"), t("deleted.nothing_to_show"))
   217	            return
   218	
   219	        popup = tk.Toplevel(self.root)
   220	        popup.title(t("deleted.details_title", ts=deleted_at))
   221	        popup.geometry("720x520")
   222	        popup.transient(self.root)
   223	        popup.update_idletasks()
   224	        popup.grab_set()
   225	
   226	        tk.Label(popup, text=t("deleted.deleted_on", ts=deleted_at),
   227	                 font=self.font(12, "bold")).pack(pady=6)
   228	
   229	        columns = ("id", "sdate", "product", "qty", "unit",
   230	                   "total", "profit", "by", "reason")
   231	        tree = ttk.Treeview(popup, columns=columns, show="headings", height=12)
   232	        headings = {
   233	            "id": (t("history.col_id"), 0),
   234	            "sdate": (t("deleted.col_sale_of"), 130),
   235	            "product": (t("history.col_product"), 180),
   236	            "qty": (t("history.col_qty"), 50),
   237	            "unit": (t("history.col_unit"), 60),
   238	            "total": (t("history.col_total"), 80),
   239	            "profit": (t("history.col_profit"), 80),
   240	            "by": (t("deleted.col_by"), 100),
   241	            "reason": (t("deleted.col_reason"), 140),
   242	        }
   243	        for col, (label, w) in headings.items():
   244	            tree.heading(col, text=label)
   245	            tree.column(col, width=w,
   246	                        anchor=(i18n.anchor_start()
   247	                                if col in ("product", "reason") else "center"),
   248	                        stretch=(col in ("product", "reason")))
   249	        tree.pack(fill="both", expand=True, padx=10, pady=(0, 5))
   250	
   251	        def rebuild():
   252	            for row in tree.get_children():
   253	                tree.delete(row)
   254	            rows = db.get_deleted_sales_by_deletion_timestamp(deleted_at)
   255	            for (sale_id, orig_date, product, qty, unit, total, profit,
   256	                 username, reason) in rows:
   257	                qty_str = (f"{qty:.0f} g" if unit in ("gram", "g")
   258	                           else f"{qty:.0f}")
   259	                tree.insert("", "end", values=(
   260	                    sale_id, orig_date, product[:28], qty_str, unit,
   261	                    f"{total:.2f} DT", f"{profit:.2f} DT",
   262	                    username, reason or "",
   263	                ))
   264	            if not rows:
   265	                messagebox.showinfo(t("msg.info"), t("deleted.no_more_items"),
   266	                                    parent=popup)
   267	                popup.destroy()
   268	                self.reload()
   269	
   270	        rebuild()
   271	
   272	        def _selected_sale_id():
   273	            sel = tree.selection()
   274	            if not sel:
   275	                messagebox.showwarning(t("msg.warning"),
   276	                                       t("deleted.select_item"), parent=popup)
   277	                return None
   278	            return tree.item(sel[0])["values"][0]
   279	
   280	        def restore_one():
   281	            sale_id = _selected_sale_id()
   282	            if sale_id is None:
   283	                return
   284	            if not messagebox.askyesno(
   285	                t("msg.confirmation"),
   286	                t("deleted.confirm_restore_one"), parent=popup):
   287	                return
   288	            try:
   289	                db.restore_sale(sale_id)
   290	            except DomainError as e:
   291	                messagebox.showerror(t("msg.error"), t(e.key, **e.kwargs),
   292	                                     parent=popup)
   293	                return
   294	            except Exception as e:
   295	                messagebox.showerror(t("msg.error"), str(e), parent=popup)
   296	                return
   297	            self.reload()
   298	            rebuild()
   299	
   300	        # NEW: permanent delete of a single line from inside the popup
   301	        def hard_delete_one():
   302	            sale_id = _selected_sale_id()
   303	            if sale_id is None:
   304	                return
   305	            if not messagebox.askyesno(
   306	                t("msg.confirmation"),
   307	                t("deleted.confirm_hard_one"), parent=popup):
   308	                return
   309	            try:
   310	                db.delete_sale(sale_id)
   311	            except DomainError as e:
   312	                messagebox.showerror(t("msg.error"), t(e.key, **e.kwargs),
   313	                                     parent=popup)
   314	                return
   315	            except Exception as e:
   316	                messagebox.showerror(t("msg.error"), str(e), parent=popup)
   317	                return
   318	            self.reload()
   319	            rebuild()
   320	
   321	        btns = tk.Frame(popup)
   322	        btns.pack(fill="x", padx=10, pady=(0, 8))
   323	        tk.Button(btns, text=t("deleted.restore_one"), bg=theme.GREEN,
   324	                  fg="white", command=restore_one
   325	                  ).pack(side=i18n.side_start(), padx=5)
   326	        if auth.can(self.user, "hard_delete_sale"):
   327	            tk.Button(btns, text=t("deleted.hard_delete_one"),
   328	                      bg=theme.RED, fg="white", command=hard_delete_one
   329	                      ).pack(side=i18n.side_start(), padx=5)
   330	        tk.Button(btns, text=t("btn.close"), bg=theme.GRAY, fg="white",
   331	                  command=popup.destroy).pack(side=i18n.side_end(), padx=5)
   332	
   333	    def on_show(self):
   334	        self.reload()

     1	"""Sales history — filter, view, soft/hard delete, verify."""
     2	import tkinter as tk
     3	from tkinter import messagebox, ttk
     4	
     5	from app import theme
     6	from database import db
     7	from database.errors import DomainError
     8	from screens.base import BaseScreen
     9	from screens.widgets import DateRangeFilter, ScrollableTreeview, build_topbar
    10	from utils import auth, i18n
    11	from utils.i18n import t
    12	
    13	
    14	class HistoryScreen(BaseScreen):
    15	    topbar_color = theme.DARK_LIGHT
    16	
    17	    def __init__(self, root, switch, user=None, logout=None):
    18	        self._active_range = (None, None)
    19	        super().__init__(root, switch, user, logout=None)
    20	        self.apply_filter()
    21	
    22	    def build_topbar(self):
    23	        start, end = [], []
    24	        if auth.can(self.user, "sell"):

    26	                          lambda: self.switch("sales")))

    28	            end.append((t("history.deleted_btn"), theme.RED_DARKER,
    29	                        lambda: self.switch("deleted")))
    30	        if auth.can(self.user, "view_reports"):
    31	            end.append((t("nav.reports_arrow"), theme.DARK,
    32	                        lambda: self.switch("reports")))
    33	        build_topbar(self.frame, color=self.topbar_color,
    34	                     title_key="history.title",
    35	                     buttons_start=start, buttons_end=end,
    36	                     logout_callback=self.logout)
    37	
    38	
    39	    def build_body(self):
    40	        main = tk.Frame(self.frame, bg=theme.BG)
    41	        main.pack(fill="both", expand=True, padx=5, pady=5)
    42	
    43	        self.filter = DateRangeFilter(
    44	            main, on_apply=self.apply_filter,
    45	            on_clear=self._clear_filter,
    46	            on_refresh=self.refresh,
    47	            show_verify=True, on_verify=self.verify_sales,
    48	        )
    49	        self.filter.pack(fill="x", pady=(0, 5))
    50	
    51	        # summary labels attached to the filter's right side
    52	        summary = tk.Frame(self.filter, bg=theme.BG_CARD)
    53	        summary.pack(side=i18n.side_end(), padx=10, pady=6)
    54	        self.total_label = tk.Label(summary, text=f'{t("lbl.total")}: 0 DT',
    55	                                    bg=theme.BG_CARD, font=self.font(10, "bold"))
    56	        self.total_label.pack(side=i18n.side_start(), padx=5)
    57	        self.profit_label = tk.Label(summary, text=f'{t("lbl.profit")}: 0 DT',
    58	                                     bg=theme.BG_CARD, fg=theme.GREEN,
    59	                                     font=self.font(10, "bold"))
    60	        self.profit_label.pack(side=i18n.side_start(), padx=5)
    61	        self.count_label = tk.Label(summary, text=f'{t("lbl.sales_count")}: 0',
    62	                                    bg=theme.BG_CARD, font=self.font(10, "bold"))
    63	        self.count_label.pack(side=i18n.side_start(), padx=5)
    64	
    65	        self.tree_widget = ScrollableTreeview(
    66	            main,

    68	            headings=[t("history.col_date"), t("history.col_time"),
    69	                      t("history.col_count"), t("history.col_total"),
    70	                      t("history.col_profit")],
    71	            widths=[110, 70, 70, 110, 110],
    72	        )
    73	        self.tree_widget.pack(fill="both", expand=True)
    74	        self.tree_widget.bind("<Double-1>", self.view_details)
    75	        self.tree = self.tree_widget.tree
    76	
    77	        self._build_footer(main)
    78	
    79	    def _build_footer(self, parent):
    80	        footer = tk.Frame(parent, bg=theme.BG)
    81	        footer.pack(fill="x", pady=5)
    82	        if auth.can(self.user, "soft_delete_sale"):
    83	            tk.Button(footer, text=t("history.btn_soft_delete"),
    84	                      bg=theme.ORANGE, fg="white",
