    87	                      ).pack(side=i18n.side_start(), padx=5)
    88	        if auth.can(self.user, "hard_delete_sale"):
    89	            tk.Button(footer, text=t("history.btn_hard_delete"),
    90	                      bg=theme.RED, fg="white",
    91	                      font=self.font(10), width=22,
    92	                      command=self.delete_selected_hard
    93	                      ).pack(side=i18n.side_start(), padx=5)
    94	        tk.Button(footer, text=t("btn.details_sale"), bg=theme.BLUE, fg="white",
    95	                  font=self.font(10), width=15,
    96	                  command=self.show_details_selected
    97	                  ).pack(side=i18n.side_start(), padx=5)
    98	        tk.Label(footer, text=t("history.hint"), bg=theme.BG, fg=theme.ORANGE,
    99	                 font=self.font(9)).pack(side=i18n.side_end(), padx=5)
   100	
   101	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   102	    def load_history(self, start_dt=None, end_dt=None):
   103	        self.tree_widget.clear()
   104	        self._active_range = (start_dt, end_dt)
   105	        groups = db.get_grouped_sales(start_dt, end_dt)
   106	        total_amount = total_profit = 0.0
   107	        for timestamp, count, amount, profit in groups:
   108	            date_part = timestamp[:10]
   109	            time_part = (timestamp[11:16] if len(timestamp) > 16
   110	                         else timestamp[11:])
   111	            self.tree_widget.insert_row(
   112	                (date_part, time_part, count, f"{amount:.2f}", f"{profit:.2f}"),
   113	                tags=(timestamp,))
   114	            total_amount += amount
   115	            total_profit += profit
   116	
   117	        self.total_label.config(text=f'{t("lbl.total")}: {total_amount:.2f} DT')
   118	        self.profit_label.config(text=f'{t("lbl.profit")}: {total_profit:.2f} DT')
   119	        self.count_label.config(text=f'{t("lbl.sales_count")}: {len(groups)}')
   120	
   121	    def apply_filter(self):
   122	        try:
   123	            start, end = self.filter.get_range()
   124	        except ValueError as e:
   125	            messagebox.showerror(t("msg.error"), str(e))
   126	            return
   127	        self.load_history(start, end)
   128	
   129	    def _clear_filter(self):
   130	        self.apply_filter()
   131	
   132	    def refresh(self):
   133	        self.load_history(*self._active_range)
   134	
   135	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   136	    def delete_selected_soft(self):
   137	        if not auth.can(self.user, "soft_delete_sale"):
   138	            messagebox.showerror(t("msg.access_denied"), t("msg.no_permission"))
   139	            return
   140	        stamps = self.tree_widget.selection_tags()
   141	        if not stamps:
   142	            messagebox.showwarning(t("msg.warning"),
   143	                                   t("history.select_to_delete"))
   144	            return
   145	        if not messagebox.askyesno(t("msg.confirmation"),
   146	                                   t("history.confirm_soft", n=len(stamps))):
   147	            return
   148	        errors = []
   149	        for ts in stamps:
   150	            try:
   151	                db.soft_delete_sales_by_timestamp(ts, self.user["id"])
   152	            except DomainError as e:
   153	                errors.append(f"{ts}: {t(e.key, **e.kwargs)}")
   154	            except Exception as e:
   155	                errors.append(f"{ts}: {e}")
   156	        self._report_bulk(errors, stamps)
   157	
   158	    def delete_selected_hard(self):
   159	        if not auth.can(self.user, "hard_delete_sale"):
   160	            messagebox.showerror(t("msg.access_denied"),
   161	                                 t("history.owner_only_hard"))
   162	            return
   163	        stamps = self.tree_widget.selection_tags()
   164	        if not stamps:
   165	            messagebox.showwarning(t("msg.warning"),
   166	                                   t("history.select_to_delete"))
   167	            return
   168	        if not messagebox.askyesno(t("msg.confirmation"),
   169	                                   t("history.confirm_hard", n=len(stamps))):
   170	            return
   171	        errors = []
   172	        for ts in stamps:
   173	            try:
   174	                db.hard_delete_sales_by_timestamp(ts)
   175	            except DomainError as e:
   176	                errors.append(f"{ts}: {t(e.key, **e.kwargs)}")
   177	            except Exception as e:
   178	                errors.append(f"{ts}: {e}")
   179	        self._report_bulk(errors, stamps)
   180	
   181	    def _report_bulk(self, errors, stamps):
   182	        if errors:
   183	            messagebox.showerror(t("msg.error"), "\n".join(errors[:5]))
   184	        else:
   185	            messagebox.showinfo(t("msg.success"),
   186	                                t("history.deleted_ok", n=len(stamps)))
   187	        self.refresh()
   188	
   189	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   190	    def show_details_selected(self):
   191	        stamps = self.tree_widget.selection_tags()
   192	        if not stamps:
   193	            messagebox.showwarning(t("msg.warning"), t("history.select_sale"))
   194	            return
   195	        self.show_details_for_timestamp(stamps[0])
   196	
   197	    def view_details(self, event=None):
   198	        sel = self.tree.selection()
   199	        if sel:
   200	            ts = self.tree.item(sel[0], "tags")[0]
   201	            self.show_details_for_timestamp(ts)
   202	
   203	    def show_details_for_timestamp(self, timestamp):
   204	        if not db.get_sales_by_timestamp(timestamp):
   205	            messagebox.showinfo(t("msg.info"), t("history.no_items"))
   206	            return
   207	
   208	        popup = tk.Toplevel(self.root)
   209	        popup.title(t("history.details_title", ts=timestamp))
   210	        popup.geometry("620x500")
   211	        popup.transient(self.root)
   212	        popup.update_idletasks()
   213	        popup.grab_set()
   214	
   215	        tk.Label(popup, text=t("history.sale_of", ts=timestamp),
   216	                 font=self.font(12, "bold")).pack(pady=6)
   217	
   218	        columns = ("id", "product", "qty", "unit", "total", "profit")
   219	        tree = ttk.Treeview(popup, columns=columns, show="headings", height=10)
   220	        for col, label, w, anchor, stretch in (
   221	            ("id", t("history.col_id"), 0, "center", False),
   222	            ("product", t("history.col_product"), 220, i18n.anchor_start(), True),
   223	            ("qty", t("history.col_qty"), 80, "center", False),
   224	            ("unit", t("history.col_unit"), 70, "center", False),
   225	            ("total", t("history.col_total"), 80, "center", False),
   226	            ("profit", t("history.col_profit"), 80, "center", False),
   227	        ):
   228	            tree.heading(col, text=label)
   229	            tree.column(col, width=w, anchor=anchor, stretch=stretch)
   230	        tree.pack(fill="both", expand=True, padx=10, pady=(0, 5))
   231	
   232	        summary = tk.Frame(popup)
   233	        summary.pack(fill="x", padx=10, pady=5)
   234	        total_lbl = tk.Label(summary, text="", font=self.font(10, "bold"))
   235	        total_lbl.pack(side=i18n.side_start(), padx=10)
   236	        profit_lbl = tk.Label(summary, text="", font=self.font(10, "bold"),
   237	                              fg=theme.GREEN)
   238	        profit_lbl.pack(side=i18n.side_start(), padx=10)
   239	
   240	        def rebuild():
   241	            for row in tree.get_children():
   242	                tree.delete(row)
   243	            rows = db.get_sales_by_timestamp(timestamp)
   244	            tot = prof = 0.0
   245	            for sale_id, product, qty, unit, total, profit in rows:
   246	                qty_str = (f"{qty:.0f} g" if unit in ("gram", "g")
   247	                           else f"{qty:.0f}")
   248	                tree.insert("", "end", values=(
   249	                    sale_id, product[:34], qty_str, unit,
   250	                    f"{total:.2f} DT", f"{profit:.2f} DT"))
   251	                tot += total
   252	                prof += profit
   253	            total_lbl.config(text=f"{t('lbl.total')}: {tot:.2f} DT")
   254	            profit_lbl.config(text=f"{t('lbl.profit')}: {prof:.2f} DT")
   255	            if not rows:
   256	                messagebox.showinfo(t("msg.info"), t("history.no_items"),
   257	                                    parent=popup)
   258	                popup.destroy()
   259	                self.refresh()
   260	
   261	        rebuild()
   262	
   263	        def delete_selected_item():
   264	            sel = tree.selection()
   265	            if not sel:
   266	                messagebox.showwarning(t("msg.warning"),
   267	                                       t("deleted.select_item"), parent=popup)
   268	                return
   269	            sale_id = tree.item(sel[0])["values"][0]
   270	            if auth.can(self.user, "hard_delete_sale"):
   271	                if not messagebox.askyesno(
   272	                    t("msg.confirmation"),
   273	                    t("history.delete_item_confirm_hard"), parent=popup):
   274	                    return
   275	                try:
   276	                    db.delete_sale(sale_id)
   277	                except Exception as e:
   278	                    messagebox.showerror(t("msg.error"), str(e), parent=popup)
   279	                    return
   280	            elif auth.can(self.user, "soft_delete_sale"):
   281	                if not messagebox.askyesno(
   282	                    t("msg.confirmation"),
   283	                    t("history.delete_item_confirm"), parent=popup):
   284	                    return
   285	                try:
   286	                    db.soft_delete_sale(sale_id, self.user["id"])
   287	                except Exception as e:
   288	                    messagebox.showerror(t("msg.error"), str(e), parent=popup)
   289	                    return
   290	            else:
   291	                messagebox.showerror(t("msg.access_denied"),
   292	                                     t("msg.no_permission"), parent=popup)
   293	                return
   294	            self.refresh()
   295	            rebuild()
   296	
   297	        btns = tk.Frame(popup)
   298	        btns.pack(fill="x", padx=10, pady=(0, 8))
   299	        if auth.can(self.user, "soft_delete_sale"):
   300	            label = (t("history.btn_hard_delete")
   301	                     if auth.can(self.user, "hard_delete_sale")
   302	                     else t("history.delete_item"))
   303	            tk.Button(btns, text=label, bg=theme.RED, fg="white",
   304	                      command=delete_selected_item
   305	                      ).pack(side=i18n.side_start(), padx=5)
   306	        tk.Button(btns, text=t("btn.close"), bg=theme.GRAY, fg="white",
   307	                  command=popup.destroy).pack(side=i18n.side_end(), padx=5)
   308	
   309	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   310	    def verify_sales(self):
   311	        at, ap, gt, gp = db.verify_sales_aggregates()
   312	        ok = abs(at - gt) < 0.01
   313	        messagebox.showinfo(
   314	            t("history.verify_title"),
   315	            "\n".join((
   316	                t("history.verify_total", t=at),
   317	                t("history.verify_grouped", t=gt),
   318	                t("history.verify_profit", p=ap),
   319	                t("history.verify_grouped_profit", p=gp),
   320	                "",
   321	                t("history.verify_ok") if ok else t("history.verify_bad"),
   322	            )),
   323	        )
   324	
   325	    def on_show(self):
   326	        self.refresh()
=================================================================

     1	"""Modal login dialog with language selector."""
     2	import tkinter as tk
     3	from tkinter import messagebox, ttk
     4	
     5	from utils import auth, i18n
     6	from utils.i18n import t
     7	
     8	
     9	class LoginDialog:
    10	    def __init__(self, root):
    11	        self.root = root
    12	        self.user = None
    13	
    14	        self.top = tk.Toplevel(root)
    15	        self.top.resizable(False, False)
    16	        self.top.configure(bg="#f0f0f0")
    17	        self._build()
    18	
    19	        self.top.bind("<Return>", lambda e: self.login())
    20	        self.top.bind("<KP_Enter>", lambda e: self.login())
    21	        self.top.protocol("WM_DELETE_WINDOW", self.cancel)
    22	
    23	        self.top.update_idletasks()
    24	        self.top.deiconify()
    25	        self.top.lift()
    26	        try:
    27	            self.top.attributes("-topmost", True)
    28	            self.top.after(250, lambda: self.top.attributes("-topmost", False))
    29	        except tk.TclError:
    30	            pass
    31	        self.username.focus_set()
    32	        try:
    33	            self.top.grab_set()
    34	        except tk.TclError:
    35	            pass
    36	
    37	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    38	    def _build(self):
    39	        for w in self.top.winfo_children():
    40	            w.destroy()
    41	
    42	        self.top.title(t("login.title"))
    43	        self.top.geometry("440x360")
    44	
    45	        tk.Label(self.top, text=t("app.title"),
    46	                 font=i18n.ui_font(16, "bold"),
    47	                 bg="#f0f0f0", fg="#2c3e50").pack(pady=(18, 2))
    48	        tk.Label(self.top, text=t("login.subtitle"),
    49	                 font=i18n.ui_font(9),
    50	                 bg="#f0f0f0", fg="#7f8c8d").pack(pady=(0, 12))
    51	
    52	        self._build_lang_selector()
    53	        self._build_credentials()
    54	
    55	        tk.Button(self.top, text=t("login.connect"),
    56	                  bg="#27ae60", fg="white",
    57	                  font=i18n.ui_font(10, "bold"), width=15,
    58	                  command=self.login).pack(pady=18)
    59	
    60	    def _build_lang_selector(self):
    61	        frame = tk.Frame(self.top, bg="#f0f0f0")
    62	        frame.pack(pady=(0, 12))
    63	        tk.Label(frame, text=t("login.language"),
    64	                 bg="#f0f0f0", fg="#2c3e50",
    65	                 font=i18n.ui_font(10)).pack(side=i18n.side_start(),
    66	                                             padx=(0, 6))
    67	
    68	        self._lang_codes = list(i18n.LANGUAGES.keys())
    69	        names = [i18n.LANGUAGES[c] for c in self._lang_codes]
    70	        current = i18n.get_language()
    71	        self.lang_var = tk.StringVar(
    72	            value=i18n.LANGUAGES.get(current, names[0]))
    73	
    74	        self.lang_combo = ttk.Combobox(
    75	            frame, textvariable=self.lang_var, values=names,
    76	            state="readonly", width=14,
    77	            font=i18n.ui_font(10), justify=i18n.justify_text(),
    78	        )
    79	        self.lang_combo.pack(side=i18n.side_start())
    80	        self.lang_combo.bind("<<ComboboxSelected>>", self._on_language_change)
    81	
    82	    def _build_credentials(self):
    83	        form = tk.Frame(self.top, bg="#f0f0f0")
    84	        form.pack(pady=6)
    85	
    86	        rtl = i18n.is_rtl()
    87	        label_col, entry_col = (1, 0) if rtl else (0, 1)
    88	        label_sticky = i18n.anchor_start()
    89	
    90	        tk.Label(form, text=t("login.username"), bg="#f0f0f0",
    91	                 font=i18n.ui_font(10)
    92	                 ).grid(row=0, column=label_col, sticky=label_sticky,
    93	                        padx=6, pady=6)
    94	        self.username = tk.Entry(form, width=22, font=i18n.ui_font(10),
    95	                                 justify=i18n.justify_text())
    96	        self.username.grid(row=0, column=entry_col, padx=6, pady=6)
    97	
    98	        tk.Label(form, text=t("login.password"), bg="#f0f0f0",
    99	                 font=i18n.ui_font(10)
   100	                 ).grid(row=1, column=label_col, sticky=label_sticky,
   101	                        padx=6, pady=6)
   102	        self.password = tk.Entry(form, width=22, show="*",
   103	                                 font=i18n.ui_font(10),
   104	                                 justify=i18n.justify_text())
   105	        self.password.grid(row=1, column=entry_col, padx=6, pady=6)
   106	
   107	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   108	    def _on_language_change(self, event=None):
   109	        for code, name in i18n.LANGUAGES.items():
   110	            if name == self.lang_var.get():
   111	                i18n.set_language(code)
   112	                break
   113	        old_user = self.username.get() if hasattr(self, "username") else ""
   114	        old_pwd = self.password.get() if hasattr(self, "password") else ""
   115	        self._build()
   116	        if old_user:
   117	            self.username.insert(0, old_user)
   118	        if old_pwd:
   119	            self.password.insert(0, old_pwd)
   120	        self.username.focus_set()
   121	
   122	    def login(self):
   123	        username = self.username.get().strip()
   124	        password = self.password.get()
   125	        if not username or not password:
   126	            messagebox.showerror(t("msg.error"), t("login.error_empty"),
   127	                                 parent=self.top)
   128	            return
   129	        user = auth.authenticate(username, password)
   130	        if not user:
   131	            messagebox.showerror(t("msg.error"), t("login.error_invalid"),
   132	                                 parent=self.top)
   133	            self.password.delete(0, tk.END)
   134	            return
   135	        self.user = user
   136	        self._close()
   137	
   138	    def cancel(self):
   139	        self.user = None
   140	        self._close()
   141	
   142	    def _close(self):
   143	        try:
   144	            self.top.grab_release()
   145	        except tk.TclError:
   146	            pass
   147	        self.top.destroy()
   148	
   149	    def show(self):
   150	        self.root.wait_window(self.top)
   151	        return self.user

   143	        self.pieces_per_pack_entry.pack(side=i18n.side_start(), ipady=2)

   146	    def _build_cigarette_fields(self, parent):
   147	        self.cigarette_frame = tk.Frame(parent, bg=theme.BG_CARD)
   148	        sub = tk.Frame(self.cigarette_frame, bg=theme.BG_CARD)
   149	        sub.pack(fill="x", padx=10, pady=3)
   150	        tk.Label(sub, text=t("products.cigarette_price"), width=14,
   151	                 anchor=i18n.anchor_start(), bg=theme.BG_CARD, fg=theme.FG,
   152	                 font=self.font(10)).pack(side=i18n.side_start())
   153	        self.single_cigarette_entry = tk.Entry(sub, width=10, font=self.font(10),
   154	                                               bg=theme.BG_INPUT, fg=theme.FG,
   155	                                               relief="solid", bd=1,
   156	                                               justify=i18n.justify_text())
   157	        self.single_cigarette_entry.pack(side=i18n.side_start(), ipady=2)
   158	        self.single_cigarette_entry.insert(0, "0")
   159	
   160	        tk.Label(self.cigarette_frame, text=t("products.cig_stock_hint"),
   161	                 bg=theme.BG_CARD, fg=theme.FG_MUTED, font=self.font(9),
   162	                 justify=i18n.justify_text()
   163	                 ).pack(pady=2, padx=10, anchor=i18n.anchor_start())
   164	
   165	    def _build_photo_and_actions(self, parent):
   166	        tk.Button(parent, text=t("btn.choose_photo"), font=self.font(10),
   167	                  bg=theme.BG_SUBTLE, fg=theme.FG, relief="flat",
   168	                  command=self.choose_photo).pack(pady=(10, 2))
   169	        self.photo_label = tk.Label(parent, text=t("products.no_photo"),
   170	                                    bg=theme.BG_CARD, fg=theme.FG_MUTED,
   171	                                    font=self.font(9))
   172	        self.photo_label.pack()
   173	
   174	        actions = tk.Frame(parent, bg=theme.BG_CARD)
   175	        actions.pack(pady=15)
   176	        tk.Button(actions, text=t("btn.add"), bg=theme.GREEN, fg="white",
   177	                  width=9, font=self.font(10, "bold"), relief="flat",
   178	                  command=self.add_product).pack(side=i18n.side_start(), padx=3)
   179	        tk.Button(actions, text=t("btn.modify"), bg=theme.ORANGE, fg="white",
   180	                  width=9, font=self.font(10, "bold"), relief="flat",
   181	                  command=self.update_product).pack(side=i18n.side_start(), padx=3)
   182	        tk.Button(actions, text=t("btn.clear"), bg=theme.GRAY, fg="white",
   183	                  width=9, font=self.font(10, "bold"), relief="flat",
   184	                  command=self.clear_all).pack(side=i18n.side_start(), padx=3)
   185	
   186	    # --- listing --------------------------------------------------------
   187	    def _build_listing(self, parent):
   188	        search = tk.Frame(parent, bg=theme.BG_CARD)
   189	        search.pack(fill="x", padx=10, pady=8)
   190	        tk.Label(search, text=t("products.search"), bg=theme.BG_CARD,
   191	                 fg=theme.FG, font=self.font(10)).pack(side=i18n.side_start())
   192	        self.search_entry = tk.Entry(search, font=self.font(10),
   193	                                     bg=theme.BG_INPUT, fg=theme.FG,
   194	                                     relief="solid", bd=1,
   195	                                     justify=i18n.justify_text())
   196	        self.search_entry.pack(side=i18n.side_start(), fill="x", expand=True,
   197	                               padx=5, ipady=2)
   198	        self.search_entry.bind("<KeyRelease>", self.search_products)
   199	
   200	        ttk.Style().configure("Products.Treeview",
   201	                              background="white", foreground="black",
   202	                              fieldbackground="white",
   203	                              font=self.font(10), rowheight=26)
   204	        ttk.Style().configure("Products.Treeview.Heading",
   205	                              font=self.font(10, "bold"),
   206	                              background=theme.BG_SUBTLE, foreground="black")
   207	
   208	        container = tk.Frame(parent, bg=theme.BG_CARD)
   209	        container.pack(fill="both", expand=True, padx=10, pady=(0, 8))
   210	
   211	        columns = ("id", "barcode", "name", "buy", "sell", "unit", "stock", "ptype")
   212	        headings = [t("products.col_id"), t("products.col_barcode"),
   213	                    t("products.col_name"), t("products.col_buy"),
   214	                    t("products.col_sell"), t("products.col_unit"),
   215	                    t("products.col_stock"), t("products.col_type")]
   216	        widths = [45, 100, 220, 80, 80, 70, 130, 80]
   217	        self.tree = ttk.Treeview(container, columns=columns, show="headings",
   218	                                 height=20, style="Products.Treeview")
   219	        for col, hdr, w in zip(columns, headings, widths):
   220	            self.tree.heading(col, text=hdr)
   221	            self.tree.column(col, width=w,
   222	                             anchor=(i18n.anchor_start()
   223	                                     if col == "name" else "center"))
   224	        v = ttk.Scrollbar(container, orient="vertical", command=self.tree.yview)
   225	        h = ttk.Scrollbar(container, orient="horizontal", command=self.tree.xview)
   226	        self.tree.configure(yscrollcommand=v.set, xscrollcommand=h.set)
   227	        self.tree.grid(row=0, column=0, sticky="nsew")
   228	        v.grid(row=0, column=1, sticky="ns")
   229	        h.grid(row=1, column=0, sticky="ew")
   230	        container.grid_rowconfigure(0, weight=1)
   231	        container.grid_columnconfigure(0, weight=1)
   232	        self.tree.bind("<<TreeviewSelect>>", self.select_product)
   233	
   234	        footer = tk.Frame(parent, bg=theme.BG_CARD)
   235	        footer.pack(fill="x", padx=10, pady=(0, 10))
   236	        tk.Button(footer, text=t("btn.delete_selection"), bg=theme.RED,
   237	                  fg="white", font=self.font(10, "bold"), relief="flat",
   238	                  command=self.delete_product
   239	                  ).pack(side=i18n.side_start(), padx=3)
   240	        tk.Button(footer, text=t("btn.add_stock"), bg=theme.BLUE, fg="white",
   241	                  font=self.font(10, "bold"), relief="flat",
   242	                  command=self.add_stock
   243	                  ).pack(side=i18n.side_start(), padx=3)
   244	
   245	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   246	    # Permissions helper
   247	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   248	    def _require_manage(self) -> bool:
   249	        if not auth.can(self.user, "manage_products"):
   250	            messagebox.showerror(t("msg.access_denied"), t("products.owner_only"))
   251	            return False
   252	        return True
   253	
   254	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   255	    # Pack / cigarette toggles
   256	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   257	    def toggle_pack_options(self):
   258	        self.cigarette_frame.pack_forget()
   259	        if self.unit_var.get() == "pack":
   260	            self.pack_frame.pack(fill="x", pady=4,
   261	                                 after=self.entries["stock"].master)
   262	        else:
   263	            self.pack_frame.pack_forget()
   264	
   265	    def toggle_cigarette_options(self):
   266	        self.pack_frame.pack_forget()
   267	        if self.unit_var.get() == "cigarette":
   268	            self.cigarette_frame.pack(fill="x", pady=4,
   269	                                      after=self.entries["stock"].master)
   270	        else:
   271	            self.cigarette_frame.pack_forget()
   272	
   273	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   274	    # Photo
   275	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   276	    def choose_photo(self):
   277	        path = filedialog.askopenfilename(
   278	            filetypes=[("Images", "*.png *.jpg *.jpeg *.gif")])
   279	        if path:
   280	            self.photo_path = path
   281	            self.photo_label.config(text=os.path.basename(path))
   282	
   283	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   284	    # Form → data
   285	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   286	    def _read_form(self, editing=False):
   287	        barcode = self.entries["barcode"].get().strip()
   288	        name = self.entries["name"].get().strip()
   289	        if not name:
   290	            raise ValueError(t("products.name_required"))
   291	        buy_price = float(self.entries["buy"].get() or "0")
   292	        sell_price = float(self.entries["sell"].get() or "0")
   293	        if sell_price <= buy_price:
   294	            raise ValueError(t("products.sell_gt_buy"))
   295	
   296	        unit = self.unit_var.get()
   297	        product_type = (self.current_product_type
   298	                        if editing and self.current_product_type
   299	                        else ("cigarette" if unit == "cigarette" else "normal"))
   300	        stock_input = float(self.entries["stock"].get() or "0")
   301	        single_price = 0.0
   302	        pieces_per_pack = 20
   303	        single_cig_price = 0.0
   304	        if unit == "pack":
   305	            single_price = float(self.single_price_entry.get() or "0")
   306	            pieces_per_pack = int(float(self.pieces_per_pack_entry.get() or "20"))
   307	            if pieces_per_pack <= 0:
   308	                raise ValueError(t("products.pieces_gt_zero"))
   309	            stock = stock_input * pieces_per_pack
   310	        elif unit == "cigarette":
   311	            single_cig_price = float(self.single_cigarette_entry.get() or "0")
   312	            stock = stock_input
   313	        else:
   314	            stock = stock_input
   315	        return {
   316	            "barcode": barcode, "name": name,
   317	            "buy_price": buy_price, "sell_price": sell_price,
   318	            "unit": unit, "stock": stock,
   319	            "single_price": single_price,
   320	            "pieces_per_pack": pieces_per_pack,
   321	            "product_type": product_type,
   322	            "single_cigarette_price": single_cig_price,
   323	        }
   324	
   325	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   326	    # CRUD
   327	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   328	    def add_product(self):
   329	        if not self._require_manage():
   330	            return
   331	        try:
   332	            data = self._read_form(editing=False)
   333	            db.add_product(photo=self.photo_path, **data)
   334	            messagebox.showinfo(t("msg.success"), t("products.success_added"))
   335	            self.clear_all()
   336	            self.load_products()
   337	        except ValueError:
   338	            messagebox.showerror(t("msg.error"), t("msg.invalid_numbers"))
   339	        except DomainError as e:
   340	            messagebox.showerror(t("msg.error"), t(e.key, **e.kwargs))
   341	        except Exception as e:
   342	            messagebox.showerror(t("msg.error"), str(e))
   343	
   344	    def update_product(self):
   345	        if not self._require_manage():
   346	            return
   347	        if not self.current_product_id:
   348	            messagebox.showwarning(t("msg.warning"), t("products.select_first"))
   349	            return
   350	        try:
   351	            data = self._read_form(editing=True)
   352	            if not self.photo_path:
   353	                for p in db.get_all_products():
   354	                    if p[0] == self.current_product_id:
   355	                        self.photo_path = p[3]
   356	                        break
   357	            db.update_product(self.current_product_id,
   358	                              photo=self.photo_path, **data)
   359	            messagebox.showinfo(t("msg.success"), t("products.success_updated"))
   360	            self.clear_all()
   361	            self.load_products()
   362	        except ValueError:
   363	            messagebox.showerror(t("msg.error"), t("msg.invalid_numbers"))
   364	        except DomainError as e:
   365	            messagebox.showerror(t("msg.error"), t(e.key, **e.kwargs))
   366	        except Exception as e:
   367	            messagebox.showerror(t("msg.error"), str(e))
   368	
   369	    def delete_product(self):
   370	        if not self._require_manage():
   371	            return
   372	        sel = self.tree.selection()
   373	        if not sel:
   374	            messagebox.showwarning(t("msg.warning"), t("products.select_first"))
   375	            return
   376	        if not messagebox.askyesno(t("msg.confirmation"),
   377	                                   t("products.confirm_delete")):
   378	            return
   379	        product_id = int(self.tree.item(sel[0])["values"][0])
   380	        try:
   381	            db.delete_product(product_id)
   382	            self.clear_all()
   383	            self.load_products()
   384	        except Exception as e:
   385	            messagebox.showerror(t("msg.error"), str(e))
   386	
   387	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   388	    # Selection / form fill
   389	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   390	    def select_product(self, event=None):
   391	        if self._loading:
   392	            return
   393	        sel = self.tree.selection()
   394	        if not sel:
   395	            return
   396	        values = self.tree.item(sel[0])["values"]
   397	        if not values:
   398	            return
   399	        try:
   400	            product_id = int(values[0])
   401	        except (ValueError, TypeError):
   402	            return
   403	        product = next((p for p in db.get_all_products() if p[0] == product_id),
   404	                       None)
   405	        if product:
   406	            self.clear_form()
   407	            self._fill_form(product)
   408	
   409	    def _fill_form(self, product):
   410	        (pid, barcode, name, _photo, buy, sell, unit, stock,
   411	         single_price, pieces_per_pack, _ptype, single_cig) = product
   412	        self.entries["barcode"].insert(0, barcode or "")
   413	        self.entries["name"].insert(0, name or "")
   414	        self.entries["buy"].insert(0, f"{buy}")
   415	        self.entries["sell"].insert(0, f"{sell}")
   416	        self.unit_var.set(unit)
   417	        if unit == "pack":
   418	            self.pack_frame.pack(fill="x", pady=4,
   419	                                 after=self.entries["stock"].master)
   420	            self.single_price_entry.delete(0, tk.END)
   421	            self.single_price_entry.insert(0, f"{single_price}")
   422	            self.pieces_per_pack_entry.delete(0, tk.END)
   423	            self.pieces_per_pack_entry.insert(0, str(pieces_per_pack))
   424	            ppp = pieces_per_pack or 1
   425	            self.entries["stock"].insert(
   426	                0, f"{stock / ppp:.2f}".rstrip("0").rstrip("."))
   427	        elif unit == "cigarette":
   428	            self.cigarette_frame.pack(fill="x", pady=4,
   429	                                      after=self.entries["stock"].master)
   430	            self.single_cigarette_entry.delete(0, tk.END)
   431	            self.single_cigarette_entry.insert(0, f"{single_cig}")
   432	            self.entries["stock"].insert(0, f"{stock:.0f}")
   433	        else:
   434	            self.entries["stock"].insert(
   435	                0, f"{stock:.2f}".rstrip("0").rstrip("."))
   436	        self.current_product_id = pid
   437	        self.current_product_type = _ptype
   438	        if _photo:
   439	            self.photo_path = _photo
   440	            self.photo_label.config(text=os.path.basename(_photo))
   441	
   442	    def clear_form(self):
   443	        for e in self.entries.values():
   444	            e.delete(0, tk.END)
   445	        self.unit_var.set("piece")
   446	        self.pack_frame.pack_forget()
   447	        self.cigarette_frame.pack_forget()
   448	        self.single_price_entry.delete(0, tk.END)
   449	        self.single_price_entry.insert(0, "0")
   450	        self.pieces_per_pack_entry.delete(0, tk.END)
   451	        self.pieces_per_pack_entry.insert(0, "20")
   452	        self.single_cigarette_entry.delete(0, tk.END)
   453	        self.single_cigarette_entry.insert(0, "0")
   454	        self.photo_path = None
   455	        self.photo_label.config(text=t("products.no_photo"))
   456	        self.current_product_id = None
   457	        self.current_product_type = None
   458	
   459	    def clear_all(self):
   460	        self.clear_form()
   461	        self.tree.selection_remove(self.tree.selection())
   462	
   463	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   464	    # Add stock dialog
   465	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   466	    def add_stock(self):
   467	        if not self._require_manage():
   468	            return
   469	        sel = self.tree.selection()
   470	        if not sel:
   471	            messagebox.showwarning(t("msg.warning"), t("products.select_first"))
   472	            return
   473	        try:
   474	            product_id = int(self.tree.item(sel[0])["values"][0])
   475	        except (ValueError, TypeError):
   476	            return
   477	        product = next((p for p in db.get_all_products() if p[0] == product_id),
   478	                       None)
   479	        if not product:
   480	            return
   481	        unit, current_stock, ppp = product[6], product[7], (product[9] or 1)
   482	
   483	        dlg = tk.Toplevel(self.root)
   484	        dlg.title(t("products.add_stock_title"))
   485	        dlg.geometry("380x340")
   486	        dlg.transient(self.root)
   487	        dlg.grab_set()
   488	        dlg.configure(bg=theme.BG)
   489	
   490	        tk.Label(dlg, text=f"{t('lbl.product')}: {product[2]}",
   491	                 font=self.font(10, "bold"),
   492	                 bg=theme.BG, fg=theme.FG).pack(pady=10)
   493	
   494	        def make_entry(label, default=None):
   495	            tk.Label(dlg, text=label, font=self.font(10),
   496	                     bg=theme.BG, fg=theme.FG).pack(pady=(8, 0))
   497	            e = tk.Entry(dlg, font=self.font(10), width=12,
   498	                         justify="center", bg="white", fg=theme.FG,
   499	                         relief="solid", bd=1)
   500	            e.pack(pady=3, ipady=3)
   501	            if default is not None:
   502	                e.insert(0, default)
   503	            return e
   504	
   505	        packs_e = pieces_e = qty_e = None
   506	        if unit == "pack":
   507	            packs = int(current_stock // ppp)
   508	            pieces = int(current_stock % ppp)
   509	            tk.Label(dlg, text=t("products.current_stock_pack",
   510	                                  packs=packs, pieces=pieces),
   511	                     font=self.font(10), bg=theme.BG, fg=theme.FG).pack()
   512	            packs_e = make_entry(t("products.add_packs"))
   513	            pieces_e = make_entry(t("products.add_pieces"), "0")
   514	            packs_e.focus()
   515	        elif unit == "cigarette":
   516	            total = int(current_stock)
   517	            tk.Label(dlg, text=t("products.current_stock_cig",
   518	                                  packs=total // 20, units=total % 20),
   519	                     font=self.font(10), bg=theme.BG, fg=theme.FG).pack()
   520	            tk.Label(dlg, text=t("products.add_cig"),
   521	                     font=self.font(10, "bold"),
   522	                     bg=theme.BG, fg=theme.FG).pack(pady=(8, 0))
   523	            tk.Label(dlg, text=t("products.add_cig_hint"), fg=theme.FG_MUTED,
   524	                     bg=theme.BG, font=self.font(9)).pack()
   525	            qty_e = make_entry(t("sales.qty"))
   526	            qty_e.focus()
   527	        elif unit == "gram":
   528	            tk.Label(dlg, text=t("products.current_stock_g", stock=current_stock),
   529	                     font=self.font(10), bg=theme.BG, fg=theme.FG).pack()
   530	            qty_e = make_entry(t("products.add_g"))
   531	            qty_e.focus()
   532	        else:
   533	            tk.Label(dlg, text=t("products.current_stock", stock=current_stock),
   534	                     font=self.font(10), bg=theme.BG, fg=theme.FG).pack()
   535	            qty_e = make_entry(t("products.qty_to_add"))
   536	            qty_e.focus()
   537	
   538	        def submit(event=None):
   539	            try:
   540	                if unit == "pack":
   541	                    add_qty = (float(packs_e.get() or "0") * ppp
   542	                               + float(pieces_e.get() or "0"))
   543	                else:
   544	                    add_qty = float(qty_e.get() or "0")
   545	                if add_qty <= 0:
   546	                    messagebox.showerror(t("msg.error"),
   547	                                         t("products.positive_number"),
   548	                                         parent=dlg)
   549	                    return
   550	                db.update_product(
   551	                    product_id, product[1], product[2], product[3],
   552	                    product[4], product[5], product[6], current_stock + add_qty,
   553	                    product[8], product[9], product[10], product[11],
   554	                )
   555	                dlg.destroy()
   556	                self.load_products()
   557	                messagebox.showinfo(t("msg.success"), t("products.stock_added"))
   558	            except ValueError:
   559	                messagebox.showerror(t("msg.error"), t("msg.invalid_numbers"),
   560	                                     parent=dlg)
   561	
   562	        for e in (packs_e, pieces_e, qty_e):
   563	            if e is not None:
   564	                e.bind("<Return>", submit)
   565	        tk.Button(dlg, text=t("btn.add"), bg=theme.GREEN, fg="white",
   566	                  font=self.font(10, "bold"), width=10, relief="flat",
   567	                  command=submit).pack(pady=15)
   568	        dlg.wait_window()
   569	
   570	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   571	    # Display helpers
   572	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   573	    def _format_stock(self, product):
   574	        unit, stock, ppp = product[6], product[7], (product[9] or 1)
   575	        if unit == "pack":
   576	            return (f"{int(stock // ppp)} {t('unit.packs_short')} + "
   577	                    f"{int(stock % ppp)} {t('unit.pieces_short')}")
   578	        if unit == "gram":
   579	            return f"{stock:g} g"
   580	        if unit == "cigarette":
   581	            total = int(stock)
   582	            return (f"{total // 20} {t('unit.packs_short')} + "
   583	                    f"{total % 20} {t('unit.pieces_short')}")
   584	        return f"{int(stock)} {t('unit.pieces')}"
   585	
   586	    def _insert_product(self, p):
   587	        self.tree.insert("", "end", values=(
   588	            p[0], p[1] or "", p[2],
   589	            f"{p[4]:.2f} DT", f"{p[5]:.2f} DT",
   590	            t(f"unit.{p[6]}"), self._format_stock(p),
   591	            p[10] if len(p) > 10 else "normal",
   592	        ))
   593	
   594	    def load_products(self):
   595	        self._loading = True
   596	        try:
   597	            for row in self.tree.get_children():
   598	                self.tree.delete(row)
   599	            for p in db.get_all_products():
   600	                self._insert_product(p)
   601	        finally:
   602	            self._loading = False
   603	
   604	    def search_products(self, event=None):
   605	        term = self.search_entry.get().strip()
   606	        if not term:
   607	            self.load_products()
   608	            return
   609	        self._loading = True
   610	        try:
   611	            for row in self.tree.get_children():
   612	                self.tree.delete(row)
   613	            for p in db.search_products_by_name(term):
   614	                self._insert_product(p)
   615	        finally:
   616	            self._loading = False
   617	
   618	    def on_show(self):
   619	        self.load_products()

     1	from .screen import ReportsScreen

     3	__all__ = ["ReportsScreen"]

FILE: screens/reports/screen.py

     1	"""Reports container — a notebook of tabs."""
     2	import tkinter as tk
     3	from tkinter import ttk
     4	
     5	from app import theme
     6	from screens.base import BaseScreen
     7	from screens.widgets import build_topbar
     8	from utils import auth, i18n
     9	from utils.i18n import t
    10	
    11	from .tab_summary import SummaryTab
    12	from .tab_products import TopProductsTab
    13	from .tab_stock import StockTab
    14	from .tab_export import ExportTab
    15	
    16	
    17	class ReportsScreen(BaseScreen):
    18	    topbar_color = theme.PURPLE
    19	
    20	    # Shared state for the tabs (mainly the low-stock threshold).
    21	    low_stock_threshold = 10
    22	
    23	    def build_topbar(self):
    24	        start, end = [], []
    25	        if auth.can(self.user, "sell"):
    26	            start.append((t("nav.back_to_sales"), theme.PURPLE_LIGHT,
    27	                          lambda: self.switch("sales")))
    28	        if auth.can(self.user, "view_history"):
    29	            start.append((t("nav.history"), theme.PURPLE_LIGHT,
    30	                          lambda: self.switch("history")))
    31	        if auth.can(self.user, "view_deleted_sales"):
    32	            start.append((t("history.deleted_btn"), theme.RED_DARKER,
    33	                          lambda: self.switch("deleted")))
    34	        if auth.can(self.user, "manage_products"):
    35	            end.append((t("nav.products_arrow"), theme.PURPLE_LIGHT,
    36	                        lambda: self.switch("products")))
    37	        if auth.can(self.user, "manage_users"):
    38	            end.append((t("nav.users"), theme.PURPLE_LIGHT,
    39	                        lambda: self.switch("users")))
    40	        build_topbar(self.frame, color=self.topbar_color,
    41	                     title_key="reports.title",
    42	                     buttons_start=start, buttons_end=end,
    43	                     logout_callback=self.logout)
    44	
    45	    def build_body(self):
    46	        nb = ttk.Notebook(self.frame)
    47	        nb.pack(fill="both", expand=True, padx=5, pady=5)
    48	
    49	        for tab_cls, key in (
    50	            (SummaryTab, "reports.tab_summary"),
    51	            (TopProductsTab, "reports.tab_products"),
    52	            (StockTab, "reports.tab_stock"),
    53	            (ExportTab, "reports.tab_export"),
    54	        ):
    55	            tab = tab_cls(nb, self)
    56	            nb.add(tab, text=t(key))
    57	
    58	    def on_show(self):
    59	        pass
=================================================================
FILE: screens/reports/tab_export.py

     1	"""Export report tab — generate, save, preview."""
     2	import os
     3	import subprocess
     4	import sys
     5	import tkinter as tk
     6	from datetime import datetime, timedelta
     7	from tkinter import messagebox, ttk
     8	
     9	from app import theme
    10	from database import db
    11	from utils import i18n, last_report
    12	from utils.i18n import t
    13	
    14	MONO_FONT = "TkFixedFont"
    15	LOW_STOCK_REPORT_CAP = 40
    16	
    17	
    18	class ExportTab(ttk.Frame):
    19	    def __init__(self, parent, screen):
    20	        super().__init__(parent)
    21	        self.screen = screen
    22	
    23	        main = ttk.Frame(self, padding=10)
    24	        main.pack(fill="both", expand=True)
    25	
    26	        box = ttk.LabelFrame(main, text=t("reports.report_period"), padding=10)
    27	        box.pack(fill="x", pady=5)
    28	
    29	        week_ago = (datetime.now() - timedelta(days=7)).strftime("%Y-%m-%d")
    30	        today = datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d")
    31	        self.start_date = self._field(box, 0, t("lbl.from"), 12, week_ago)
    32	        self.start_time = self._field(box, 0, None, 6, "00:00", col=2)
    33	        self.end_date = self._field(box, 1, t("lbl.to"), 12, today)
    34	        self.end_time = self._field(box, 1, None, 6, "23:59", col=2)
    35	
    36	        btns = ttk.Frame(box)
    37	        btns.grid(row=2, column=0, columnspan=3, pady=10)
    38	        ttk.Button(btns, text=t("reports.generate"),
    39	                   command=self.generate).pack(side="left", padx=5)
    40	        ttk.Button(btns, text=t("reports.from_last"),
    41	                   command=self.set_from_last).pack(side="left", padx=5)
    42	        ttk.Button(btns, text=t("reports.open_folder"),
    43	                   command=self.open_folder).pack(side="left", padx=5)
    44	
    45	        wrap = ttk.LabelFrame(main, text=t("reports.preview"), padding=5)
    46	        wrap.pack(fill="both", expand=True, pady=10)
    47	        inner = ttk.Frame(wrap)
    48	        inner.pack(fill="both", expand=True)
    49	
    50	        self.preview = tk.Text(inner, height=14, bg=theme.BG_INPUT, fg=theme.FG,
    51	                               insertbackground=theme.FG, font=MONO_FONT,
    52	                               wrap="none")
    53	        v = ttk.Scrollbar(inner, orient="vertical", command=self.preview.yview)
    54	        h = ttk.Scrollbar(inner, orient="horizontal", command=self.preview.xview)
    55	        self.preview.configure(yscrollcommand=v.set, xscrollcommand=h.set)
    56	        self.preview.grid(row=0, column=0, sticky="nsew")
    57	        v.grid(row=0, column=1, sticky="ns")
    58	        h.grid(row=1, column=0, sticky="ew")
    59	        inner.grid_rowconfigure(0, weight=1)
    60	        inner.grid_columnconfigure(0, weight=1)
    61	
    62	    def _field(self, parent, row, label, width, default, col=1):
    63	        if label:
    64	            ttk.Label(parent, text=label).grid(row=row, column=0,
    65	                                                sticky="e", padx=5, pady=5)
    66	        e = ttk.Entry(parent, width=width, justify=i18n.justify_text())
    67	        e.grid(row=row, column=col, padx=5, pady=5)
    68	        e.insert(0, default)
    69	        return e
    70	
    71	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    72	    @staticmethod
    73	    def _reports_dir() -> str:
    74	        return os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(
    75	            os.path.abspath(__file__)))), "reports")
    76	
    77	    def set_from_last(self):
    78	        last_end = last_report.load_last_report()
    79	        if not last_end:
    80	            messagebox.showinfo(t("msg.info"), t("reports.no_previous"))
    81	            return
    82	        try:
    83	            dt = (datetime.strptime(last_end, "%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")
    84	                  + timedelta(seconds=1))
    85	        except ValueError:
    86	            messagebox.showerror(t("msg.error"), t("reports.corrupt_last"))
    87	            return
    88	        now = datetime.now()
    89	        for e, v in ((self.start_date, dt.strftime("%Y-%m-%d")),
    90	                     (self.start_time, dt.strftime("%H:%M")),
    91	                     (self.end_date, now.strftime("%Y-%m-%d")),
    92	                     (self.end_time, now.strftime("%H:%M"))):
    93	            e.delete(0, tk.END)
    94	            e.insert(0, v)
    95	
    96	    def open_folder(self):
    97	        d = self._reports_dir()
    98	        if not os.path.exists(d):
    99	            messagebox.showinfo(t("msg.info"), t("reports.no_reports_yet"))
   100	            return
   101	        try:
   102	            if sys.platform.startswith("linux"):
   103	                subprocess.Popen(["xdg-open", d])
   104	            elif sys.platform == "darwin":
   105	                subprocess.Popen(["open", d])
   106	            else:
   107	                os.startfile(d)  # noqa: S606
   108	        except Exception as e:
   109	            messagebox.showerror(t("msg.error"), t("reports.cannot_open", e=e))
   110	
   111	    def generate(self):
   112	        try:
   113	            start_dt = (f"{self.start_date.get().strip()} "
   114	                        f"{self.start_time.get().strip()}:00")
   115	            end_dt = (f"{self.end_date.get().strip()} "
   116	                      f"{self.end_time.get().strip()}:59")
   117	            datetime.strptime(start_dt, "%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")
   118	            datetime.strptime(end_dt, "%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")
   119	        except ValueError:
   120	            messagebox.showerror(t("msg.error"), t("reports.invalid_datetime"))
   121	            return
   122	        if start_dt > end_dt:
   123	            messagebox.showerror(t("msg.error"), t("reports.start_after_end"))
   124	            return
   125	
   126	        report = self._build_report(start_dt, end_dt)
   127	        self.preview.delete(1.0, tk.END)
   128	        self.preview.insert(1.0, report)
   129	
   130	        path = self._save(report, start_dt, end_dt)
   131	        if not path:
   132	            return
   133	        last_report.save_last_report(end_dt)
   134	        messagebox.showinfo(t("msg.success"),
   135	                            t("reports.report_saved", path=path))
   136	
   137	    def _build_report(self, start_dt, end_dt) -> str:
   138	        total, profit, count = db.get_period_summary_datetime(start_dt, end_dt)
   139	        top = db.get_top_products_datetime(10, start_dt, end_dt)
   140	        threshold = self.screen.low_stock_threshold
   141	        total_value, low_stock = db.get_stock_status(threshold)
   142	
   143	        lines = [
   144	            "=" * 70,
   145	            t("reports.report_title"),
   146	            "=" * 70,
   147	            t("reports.report_from", d=start_dt),
   148	            t("reports.report_to", d=end_dt),
   149	            t("reports.report_generated",
   150	              d=datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")),
   151	            t("reports.report_by",
   152	              u=self.screen.user["username"] if self.screen.user else "?"),
   153	            "",
   154	            t("reports.report_sales_summary"),
   155	            "-" * 50,
   156	            t("reports.report_total_sales", v=total),
   157	            t("reports.report_total_profit", v=profit),
   158	            t("reports.report_num_trans", n=count),
   159	            "",
   160	            t("reports.report_top10"),
   161	            "-" * 50,
   162	        ]
   163	        if top:
   164	            for i, (name, qty, revenue, prof) in enumerate(top, 1):
   165	                lines.append(f"{i:>2}. {name[:32]:32}  {qty:>6g} pcs  "
   166	                             f"{revenue:>9.2f} DT  {prof:>9.2f} DT")
   167	        else:
   168	            lines.append(t("reports.report_no_sales"))
   169	
   170	        lines += [
   171	            "", t("reports.report_stock"), "-" * 50,
   172	            t("reports.report_stock_value", v=total_value),
   173	            "", t("reports.report_low_stock", t=threshold),
   174	        ]
   175	        if not low_stock:
   176	            lines.append(t("reports.report_none"))
   177	        else:
   178	            for name, stock, unit in low_stock[:LOW_STOCK_REPORT_CAP]:
   179	                lines.append(f"  • {name} : {stock:g} {t(f'unit.{unit}')}")
   180	            hidden = len(low_stock) - LOW_STOCK_REPORT_CAP
   181	            if hidden > 0:
   182	                lines.append(t("reports.report_hidden", n=hidden))
   183	
   184	        lines += ["", "=" * 70, t("reports.report_success"), "=" * 70, ""]
   185	        return "\n".join(lines)
   186	
   187	    def _save(self, report, start_dt, end_dt) -> str | None:
   188	        d = self._reports_dir()
   189	        os.makedirs(d, exist_ok=True)
   190	        safe_start = start_dt.replace(" ", "_").replace(":", "")
   191	        safe_end = end_dt.replace(" ", "_").replace(":", "")
   192	        path = os.path.join(d, f"rapport_{safe_start}_{safe_end}.txt")
   193	        counter = 1
   194	        while os.path.exists(path):
   195	            path = os.path.join(
   196	                d, f"rapport_{safe_start}_{safe_end}_{counter}.txt")
   197	            counter += 1
   198	        try:
   199	            with open(path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
   200	                f.write(report)
   201	        except OSError as e:
   202	            messagebox.showerror(t("msg.error"),
   203	                                 t("reports.cannot_write", e=e))
   204	            return None
   205	        return path

     1	"""Top products tab."""
     2	from datetime import datetime
     3	from tkinter import messagebox, ttk
     4	
     5	from app import theme
     6	from database import db
     7	from utils import i18n
     8	from utils.i18n import t
     9	
    10	
    11	class TopProductsTab(ttk.Frame):
    12	    def __init__(self, parent, screen):
    13	        super().__init__(parent)
    14	        self.screen = screen
    15	
    16	        main = ttk.Frame(self, padding=10)
    17	        main.pack(fill="both", expand=True)
    18	
    19	        box = ttk.LabelFrame(main, text=t("reports.period_optional"), padding=10)
    20	        box.pack(fill="x", pady=5)
    21	
    22	        self.start_date = self._field(box, 0, t("lbl.from"), 12)
    23	        self.start_time = self._field(box, 0, None, 6, col=2)
    24	        self.end_date = self._field(box, 1, t("lbl.to"), 12)
    25	        self.end_time = self._field(box, 1, None, 6, col=2)
    26	
    27	        limit = ttk.Frame(box)
    28	        limit.grid(row=2, column=0, columnspan=3, pady=5)
    29	        ttk.Label(limit, text=t("reports.num_products")).pack(side="left", padx=5)
    30	        self.limit = ttk.Entry(limit, width=5, justify="center")
    31	        self.limit.pack(side="left", padx=5)
    32	        self.limit.insert(0, "10")
    33	
    34	        btns = ttk.Frame(box)
    35	        btns.grid(row=3, column=0, columnspan=3, pady=10)
    36	        ttk.Button(btns, text=t("reports.load_top"),
    37	                   command=self.load).pack(side="left", padx=5)
    38	        ttk.Button(btns, text=t("reports.clear_period"),
    39	                   command=self.clear_period).pack(side="left", padx=5)
    40	
    41	        ttk.Style().configure("Reports.Treeview",
    42	                              background="white", foreground="black",
    43	                              fieldbackground="white",
    44	                              font=i18n.ui_font(10), rowheight=26)
    45	        ttk.Style().configure("Reports.Treeview.Heading",
    46	                              font=i18n.ui_font(10, "bold"),
    47	                              background=theme.BG_SUBTLE, foreground="black")
    48	
    49	        wrap = ttk.Frame(main)
    50	        wrap.pack(fill="both", expand=True, pady=10)
    51	        columns = ("product", "qty", "revenue", "profit")
    52	        headings = [t("reports.col_product"), t("reports.col_qty"),
    53	                    t("reports.col_revenue"), t("reports.col_profit")]
    54	        self.tree = ttk.Treeview(wrap, columns=columns, show="headings",
    55	                                 height=15, style="Reports.Treeview")
    56	        for col, hdr in zip(columns, headings):
    57	            self.tree.heading(col, text=hdr)
    58	            self.tree.column(col,
    59	                             width=200 if col == "product" else 110,
    60	                             anchor=(i18n.anchor_start()
    61	                                     if col == "product" else "center"))
    62	        v = ttk.Scrollbar(wrap, orient="vertical", command=self.tree.yview)
    63	        self.tree.configure(yscrollcommand=v.set)
    64	        self.tree.pack(side="left", fill="both", expand=True)
    65	        v.pack(side="right", fill="y")
    66	
    67	    def _field(self, parent, row, label, width, default="", col=1):
    68	        if label:
    69	            ttk.Label(parent, text=label).grid(row=row, column=0,
    70	                                                sticky="e", padx=5, pady=5)
    71	        e = ttk.Entry(parent, width=width, justify=i18n.justify_text())
    72	        e.grid(row=row, column=col, padx=5, pady=5)
    73	        if default:
    74	            e.insert(0, default)
    75	        return e
    76	
    77	    def clear_period(self):
    78	        for e in (self.start_date, self.start_time,
    79	                  self.end_date, self.end_time):
    80	            e.delete(0, tk.END)
    81	
    82	    def load(self):
    83	        for row in self.tree.get_children():
    84	            self.tree.delete(row)
    85	        try:
    86	            limit = int(self.limit.get() or "10")
    87	        except ValueError:
    88	            limit = 10
    89	
    90	        start_dt = end_dt = None
    91	        if self.start_date.get().strip() and self.start_time.get().strip():
    92	            try:
    93	                start_dt = (f"{self.start_date.get().strip()} "
    94	                            f"{self.start_time.get().strip()}:00")
    95	                datetime.strptime(start_dt, "%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")
    96	            except ValueError:
    97	                messagebox.showerror(t("msg.error"), t("reports.invalid_datetime"))
    98	                return
    99	        if self.end_date.get().strip() and self.end_time.get().strip():
   100	            try:
   101	                end_dt = (f"{self.end_date.get().strip()} "
   102	                          f"{self.end_time.get().strip()}:59")
   103	                datetime.strptime(end_dt, "%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")
   104	            except ValueError:
   105	                messagebox.showerror(t("msg.error"), t("reports.invalid_datetime"))
   106	                return
   107	        if start_dt and end_dt and start_dt > end_dt:
   108	            messagebox.showerror(t("msg.error"), t("reports.start_after_end"))
   109	            return
   110	
   111	        for name, qty, revenue, profit in db.get_top_products_datetime(
   112	                limit, start_dt, end_dt):
   113	            self.tree.insert("", "end", values=(
   114	                name[:45], f"{qty:g}", f"{revenue:.2f}", f"{profit:.2f}"))

     1	"""Stock status tab."""
     2	import tkinter as tk
     3	from tkinter import ttk
     4	
     5	from app import theme
     6	from database import db
     7	from utils.i18n import t
     8	
     9	MONO_FONT = "TkFixedFont"
    10	
    11	
    12	class StockTab(ttk.Frame):
    13	    def __init__(self, parent, screen):
    14	        super().__init__(parent)
    15	        self.screen = screen
    16	
    17	        main = ttk.Frame(self, padding=10)
    18	        main.pack(fill="both", expand=True)
    19	
    20	        ctrl = ttk.Frame(main)
    21	        ctrl.pack(fill="x", pady=5)
    22	        ttk.Label(ctrl, text=t("reports.low_stock_threshold")).pack(side="left",
    23	                                                                   padx=5)
    24	        self.threshold = ttk.Entry(ctrl, width=5, justify="center")
    25	        self.threshold.pack(side="left", padx=5)
    26	        self.threshold.insert(0, str(self.screen.low_stock_threshold))
    27	        ttk.Button(ctrl, text=t("reports.refresh_btn"),
    28	                   command=self.load).pack(side="left", padx=10)
    29	
    30	        wrap = ttk.Frame(main)
    31	        wrap.pack(fill="both", expand=True, pady=10)
    32	        self.text = tk.Text(wrap, height=15, bg=theme.BG_INPUT, fg=theme.FG,
    33	                            insertbackground=theme.FG, font=MONO_FONT,
    34	                            relief="sunken", bd=1, wrap="none")
    35	        v = ttk.Scrollbar(wrap, orient="vertical", command=self.text.yview)
    36	        self.text.configure(yscrollcommand=v.set)
    37	        self.text.pack(side="left", fill="both", expand=True)
    38	        v.pack(side="right", fill="y")
    39	
    40	    def _threshold(self) -> int:
    41	        try:
    42	            value = int(self.threshold.get())
    43	            if value < 0:
    44	                raise ValueError
    45	            return value
    46	        except (ValueError, AttributeError):
    47	            return self.screen.low_stock_threshold
    48	
    49	    def load(self):
    50	        threshold = self._threshold()
    51	        self.screen.low_stock_threshold = threshold
    52	        total_value, low_stock = db.get_stock_status(threshold)
    53	
    54	        self.text.delete(1.0, tk.END)
    55	        self.text.insert(1.0, t("reports.stock_value", v=total_value) + "\n\n"
    56	                              + t("reports.low_stock_header", t=threshold) + "\n")
    57	        if low_stock:
    58	            for name, stock, unit in low_stock:
    59	                self.text.insert(
    60	                    tk.END, f"  • {name} : {stock:g} {t(f'unit.{unit}')}\n")
    61	        else:
    62	            self.text.insert(tk.END, t("reports.no_low_stock") + "\n")

FILE: screens/reports/tab_summary.py

     1	"""Sales summary tab."""
     2	import tkinter as tk
     3	from datetime import datetime
     4	from tkinter import messagebox, ttk
     5	
     6	from app import theme
     7	from database import db
     8	from utils import i18n
     9	from utils.i18n import t
    10	
    11	MONO_FONT = "TkFixedFont"
    12	
    13	
    14	class SummaryTab(ttk.Frame):
    15	    def __init__(self, parent, screen):
    16	        super().__init__(parent)
    17	        self.screen = screen
    18	
    19	        main = ttk.Frame(self, padding=10)
    20	        main.pack(fill="both", expand=True)
    21	
    22	        box = ttk.LabelFrame(main, text=t("reports.period"), padding=10)
    23	        box.pack(fill="x", pady=5)
    24	
    25	        today = datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d")
    26	        self.start_date = self._field(box, 0, t("lbl.from"), 12, today)
    27	        self.start_time = self._field(box, 0, None, 6, "00:00", col=2)
    28	        self.end_date = self._field(box, 1, t("lbl.to"), 12, today)
    29	        self.end_time = self._field(box, 1, None, 6, "23:59", col=2)
    30	
    31	        btns = ttk.Frame(box)
    32	        btns.grid(row=2, column=0, columnspan=3, pady=10)
    33	        ttk.Button(btns, text=t("reports.load_summary"),
    34	                   command=self.load).pack(side="left", padx=5)
    35	        ttk.Button(btns, text=t("reports.today"),
    36	                   command=self.set_today).pack(side="left", padx=5)
    37	
    38	        wrap = ttk.Frame(main)
    39	        wrap.pack(fill="both", expand=True, pady=10)
    40	        self.text = tk.Text(wrap, height=10, bg=theme.BG_INPUT, fg=theme.FG,
    41	                            insertbackground=theme.FG, font=MONO_FONT,
    42	                            relief="sunken", bd=1, wrap="none")
    43	        v = ttk.Scrollbar(wrap, orient="vertical", command=self.text.yview)
    44	        self.text.configure(yscrollcommand=v.set)
    45	        self.text.pack(side="left", fill="both", expand=True)
    46	        v.pack(side="right", fill="y")
    47	
    48	    def _field(self, parent, row, label, width, default, col=1):
    49	        if label:
    50	            ttk.Label(parent, text=label).grid(row=row, column=0,
    51	                                                sticky="e", padx=5, pady=5)
    52	        e = ttk.Entry(parent, width=width, justify=i18n.justify_text())
    53	        e.grid(row=row, column=col, padx=5, pady=5)
    54	        e.insert(0, default)
    55	        return e
    56	
    57	    def set_today(self):
    58	        today = datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d")
    59	        for e, v in ((self.start_date, today), (self.start_time, "00:00"),
    60	                     (self.end_date, today), (self.end_time, "23:59")):
    61	            e.delete(0, tk.END)
    62	            e.insert(0, v)
    63	
    64	    def load(self):
    65	        try:
    66	            start = f"{self.start_date.get().strip()} {self.start_time.get().strip()}:00"
    67	            end = f"{self.end_date.get().strip()} {self.end_time.get().strip()}:59"
    68	            datetime.strptime(start, "%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")
    69	            datetime.strptime(end, "%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")
    70	        except ValueError:
    71	            messagebox.showerror(t("msg.error"), t("reports.invalid_datetime"))
    72	            return
    73	        if start > end:
    74	            messagebox.showerror(t("msg.error"), t("reports.start_after_end"))
    75	            return
    76	
    77	        total, profit, count = db.get_period_summary_datetime(start, end)
    78	        self.text.delete(1.0, tk.END)
    79	        self.text.insert(1.0, (
    80	            f"{'=' * 52}\n"
    81	            f"  {t('reports.summary_header', start=start, end=end)}\n"
    82	            f"{'=' * 52}\n"
    83	            f"  {t('reports.total_sales')}      : {total:>14.2f} DT\n"
    84	            f"  {t('reports.total_profit')}    : {profit:>14.2f} DT\n"
    85	            f"  {t('reports.transactions')}      : {count:>14}\n"
    86	            f"{'=' * 52}\n"
    87	        ))

   244	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   245	    # Tooltip
   246	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   247	    def _attach_tooltip(self, widget, text):
   248	        def enter(_):
   249	            x = widget.winfo_rootx() + 20
   250	            y = widget.winfo_rooty() + 20
   251	            self.tooltip = tk.Toplevel(widget)
   252	            self.tooltip.wm_overrideredirect(True)
   253	            self.tooltip.wm_geometry(f"+{x}+{y}")
   254	            tk.Label(self.tooltip, text=text, bg="#ffffe0",
   255	                     relief="solid", bd=1).pack()
   256	
   257	        def leave(_):
   258	            if self.tooltip and self.tooltip.winfo_exists():
   259	                self.tooltip.destroy()
   260	                self.tooltip = None
   261	
   262	        widget.bind("<Enter>", enter)
   263	        widget.bind("<Leave>", leave)
