   265	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   266	    # Telecom products
   267	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   268	    def load_telecom_products(self):
   269	        self.telecom_products = db.get_telecom_products()
   270	        self.telecom_by_name = {p[1]: p for p in self.telecom_products}
   271	
   272	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   273	    # Keyboard shortcuts
   274	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   275	    def setup_keyboard_shortcuts(self):
   276	        self._unbind_shortcuts()
   277	        for key in TELECOM_SHORTCUTS:
   278	            self.frame.bind_all(f"<{key}>", self.on_telecom_key)
   279	        self.frame.bind_all("<space>", self.on_pay_key)
   280	        self.frame.bind_all("<Escape>", self.on_escape)
   281	
   282	    def _unbind_shortcuts(self):
   283	        for key in TELECOM_SHORTCUTS:
   284	            self.frame.unbind_all(f"<{key}>")
   285	        self.frame.unbind_all("<space>")
   286	        self.frame.unbind_all("<Escape>")
   287	
   288	    def _barcode_focused(self):
   289	        try:
   290	            return self.root.focus_get() is self.barcode_entry
   291	        except (KeyError, tk.TclError):
   292	            return False
   293	
   294	    def on_telecom_key(self, event):
   295	        if self.state == "POPUP_ACTIVE":
   296	            return
   297	        name = TELECOM_SHORTCUTS.get(event.keysym)
   298	        if name:
   299	            self.add_telecom_to_cart(name)
   300	        return "break"
   301	
   302	    def on_barcode_space(self, event):
   303	        if self.barcode_entry.get() == "":
   304	            if self.cart:
   305	                self.complete_sale()
   306	            return "break"
   307	        return None
   308	
   309	    def on_pay_key(self, event):
   310	        if self.state in ("POPUP_ACTIVE", "SEARCHING"):
   311	            return
   312	        if self._barcode_focused():
   313	            return
   314	        if self.cart:
   315	            self.complete_sale()
   316	        return "break"
   317	
   318	    def on_escape(self, event):
   319	        if self.state == "POPUP_ACTIVE":
   320	            self.on_popup_close()
   321	        elif self.state == "SEARCHING":
   322	            self._dismiss_search()
   323	            self.barcode_entry.delete(0, tk.END)
   324	            self.set_state("SCAN")
   325	        return "break"
   326	
   327	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   328	    # State machine
   329	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   330	    def set_state(self, new_state):
   331	        self.state = new_state
   332	        colors = {"SCAN": theme.DARK_LIGHT,
   333	                  "SEARCHING": theme.ORANGE,
   334	                  "POPUP_ACTIVE": theme.PURPLE_LIGHT}
   335	        labels = {"SCAN": "sales.state_scan",
   336	                  "SEARCHING": "sales.state_searching",
   337	                  "POPUP_ACTIVE": "sales.state_popup"}
   338	        color = colors.get(new_state, theme.DARK_LIGHT)
   339	        self.state_label.config(text=t(labels.get(new_state, "sales.state_scan")),
   340	                                bg=color)
   341	        self.state_frame.config(bg=color)
   342	
   343	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   344	    # Scan / search
   345	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   346	    def on_barcode_key(self, event):
   347	        if self.after_id:
   348	            self.frame.after_cancel(self.after_id)
   349	            self.after_id = None
   350	        if self.state == "POPUP_ACTIVE":
   351	            return
   352	        if event.keysym == "Down" and self.search_frame.winfo_ismapped():
   353	            self.focus_search_results()
   354	            return
   355	        value = self.barcode_entry.get()
   356	        if value.isdigit():
   357	            self.set_state("SCAN")
   358	            self._dismiss_search()
   359	            return
   360	        if len(value) >= 1:
   361	            self.set_state("SEARCHING")
   362	            self.after_id = self.frame.after(
   363	                250, lambda: self.search_products(value))
   364	        else:
   365	            self.set_state("SCAN")
   366	            self._dismiss_search()
   367	
   368	    def focus_search_results(self, event=None):
   369	        if self.search_frame.winfo_ismapped() and self.search_listbox.size() > 0:
   370	            self.search_listbox.focus_set()
   371	            self.search_listbox.selection_clear(0, tk.END)
   372	            self.search_listbox.selection_set(0)
   373	            return "break"
   374	
   375	    @staticmethod
   376	    def validate_numeric(char):
   377	        return char.isdigit() or char == ""
   378	
   379	    @staticmethod
   380	    def validate_decimal(char):
   381	        return char.isdigit() or char == "."
   382	
   383	    def search_products(self, term):
   384	        term = term.strip()
   385	        if not term:
   386	            self._dismiss_search()
   387	            return
   388	        products = db.search_products_by_name(term)
   389	        self.search_results = products
   390	        self.search_listbox_products = products
   391	        if not products:
   392	            self._dismiss_search()
   393	            return
   394	        self.search_listbox.delete(0, tk.END)
   395	        for p in products[:8]:
   396	            self.search_listbox.insert(tk.END, f"{p[2][:30]}  —  {p[5]:.2f} DT")
   397	        self.search_frame.pack(fill="x", pady=(0, 5),
   398	                               after=self.barcode_entry.master)
   399	
   400	    def _dismiss_search(self):
   401	        self.search_frame.pack_forget()
   402	        self.search_results = []
   403	        self.search_listbox_products = []
   404	
   405	    def on_search_click(self, event):
   406	        self.select_from_search()
   407	
   408	    def select_from_search(self, event=None):
   409	        sel = self.search_listbox.curselection()
   410	        if not sel or not self.search_listbox_products:
   411	            return
   412	        idx = sel[0]
   413	        if idx >= len(self.search_listbox_products):
   414	            return
   415	        product = self.search_listbox_products[idx]
   416	        self._dismiss_search()
   417	        self.barcode_entry.delete(0, tk.END)
   418	        self.set_state("SCAN")
   419	        self._open_product_popup(product)
   420	
   421	    def process_barcode(self, event=None):
   422	        barcode = self.barcode_entry.get().strip()
   423	        if not barcode:
   424	            return
   425	        product = db.get_product_by_barcode(barcode)
   426	        self.barcode_entry.delete(0, tk.END)
   427	        if not product:
   428	            self.show_notification(t("sales.not_found"), is_error=True)
   429	            return
   430	        self._open_product_popup(product)
   431	
   432	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   433	    # Product popups
   434	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   435	    def _open_product_popup(self, product):
   436	        ptype = product[10] if len(product) > 10 else "normal"
   437	        unit = product[6]
   438	        if ptype == "cigarette":
   439	            self.show_cigarette_popup(product)
   440	        elif unit == "gram":
   441	            self.show_gram_popup(product)
   442	        elif unit == "pack":
   443	            self.show_pack_popup(product)
   444	        else:
   445	            ok = self.add_to_cart(product, 1, "piece", 1,
   446	                                  product[5], product[5] - product[4])
   447	            if ok:
   448	                self.show_notification(t("sales.added", name=product[2]))
   449	
   450	    def _quantity_popup(self, product, *, title, header_lines,
   451	                        unit_options, default_unit, compute, stock_check=None):
   452	        if self.active_popup and self.active_popup.winfo_exists():
   453	            self.active_popup.destroy()
   454	        self.set_state("POPUP_ACTIVE")
   455	        self._unbind_shortcuts()
   456	
   457	        popup = tk.Toplevel(self.root)
   458	        popup.title(title)
   459	        popup.geometry(f"{theme.DIALOG_W}x{theme.DIALOG_H}")
   460	        popup.transient(self.root)
   461	        popup.grab_set()
   462	        popup.focus_force()
   463	        popup.configure(bg=theme.BG)
   464	        self.active_popup = popup
   465	
   466	        x = self.barcode_entry.winfo_rootx()
   467	        y = self.barcode_entry.winfo_rooty() + self.barcode_entry.winfo_height() + 5
   468	        popup.geometry(f"+{x}+{y}")
   469	
   470	        tk.Label(popup, text="\n".join(header_lines),
   471	                 font=self.font(11, "bold"),
   472	                 bg=theme.BG, fg=theme.FG,
   473	                 justify="center").pack(pady=8)
   474	
   475	        unit_var = tk.StringVar(value=default_unit)
   476	        unit_frame = tk.Frame(popup, bg=theme.BG)
   477	        unit_frame.pack(pady=4)
   478	        tk.Label(unit_frame, text=t("sales.sell_in"), font=self.font(10),
   479	                 bg=theme.BG, fg=theme.FG
   480	                 ).pack(side=i18n.side_start(), padx=5)
   481	        for label, value in unit_options:
   482	            tk.Radiobutton(unit_frame, text=label, variable=unit_var,
   483	                           value=value, font=self.font(10),
   484	                           bg=theme.BG, fg=theme.FG,
   485	                           activebackground=theme.BG,
   486	                           selectcolor=theme.BG
   487	                           ).pack(side=i18n.side_start(), padx=4)
   488	
   489	        tk.Label(popup, text=t("sales.qty"), font=self.font(10),
   490	                 bg=theme.BG, fg=theme.FG).pack(pady=(8, 2))
   491	        vcmd = (popup.register(self.validate_decimal), "%S")
   492	        entry = tk.Entry(popup, font=self.font(16), width=12,
   493	                         justify="center", validate="key",
   494	                         validatecommand=vcmd,
   495	                         bg="white", fg=theme.FG, relief="solid", bd=1)
   496	        entry.pack(pady=4, ipady=4)
   497	        entry.focus()
   498	        entry.select_range(0, tk.END)
   499	
   500	        preview_frame = tk.Frame(popup, bg=theme.BG)
   501	        preview_frame.pack(pady=6)
   502	        tk.Label(preview_frame, text=t("lbl.total") + ": ",
   503	                 font=self.font(12), bg=theme.BG, fg=theme.FG
   504	                 ).pack(side=i18n.side_start())
   505	        preview_label = tk.Label(preview_frame, text="0.00 DT",
   506	                                 font=self.font(12, "bold"),
   507	                                 bg=theme.BG, fg=theme.GREEN)
   508	        preview_label.pack(side=i18n.side_start())
   509	
   510	        warning_label = tk.Label(popup, text="", font=self.font(9),
   511	                                 bg=theme.BG, fg=theme.RED)
   512	        warning_label.pack()
   513	
   514	        def update_preview(*_):
   515	            warning_label.config(text="")
   516	            try:
   517	                qty = float(entry.get() or "0")
   518	                result = compute(qty, unit_var.get())
   519	                preview_label.config(text=f"{result['total']:.2f} DT")
   520	                if stock_check:
   521	                    msg = stock_check(qty, unit_var.get())
   522	                    if msg:
   523	                        warning_label.config(text=msg)
   524	            except (ValueError, KeyError):
   525	                preview_label.config(text="0.00 DT")
   526	
   527	        entry.bind("<KeyRelease>", update_preview)
   528	        unit_var.trace_add("write", update_preview)
   529	
   530	        tk.Label(popup, text=t("sales.popup_hint"),
   531	                 fg=theme.FG_MUTED, bg=theme.BG,
   532	                 font=self.font(9)).pack(pady=4)
   533	
   534	        def cycle_unit():
   535	            values = [v for _, v in unit_options]
   536	            idx = values.index(unit_var.get())
   537	            unit_var.set(values[(idx + 1) % len(values)])
   538	
   539	        def do_add():
   540	            try:
   541	                qty = float(entry.get() or "0")
   542	            except ValueError:
   543	                return
   544	            if qty <= 0:
   545	                return
   546	            if stock_check:
   547	                msg = stock_check(qty, unit_var.get())
   548	                if msg:
   549	                    messagebox.showerror(t("msg.error"), msg, parent=popup)
   550	                    return
   551	            try:
   552	                result = compute(qty, unit_var.get())
   553	            except (ValueError, KeyError):
   554	                return
   555	            ok = self.add_to_cart(
   556	                product, result["pieces_qty"], result["unit_display"],
   557	                result["display_qty"], result["total"], result["profit"],
   558	            )
   559	            if ok:
   560	                self.show_notification(t("sales.added_qty",
   561	                                          qty=result["display_qty"],
   562	                                          unit=result["unit_display"]))
   563	            popup.destroy()
   564	            self.on_popup_close()
   565	
   566	        popup.bind("<Key-u>", lambda e: cycle_unit())
   567	        popup.bind("<Key-U>", lambda e: cycle_unit())
   568	        popup.bind("<Return>", lambda e: do_add())
   569	        popup.bind("<KP_Enter>", lambda e: do_add())
   570	        popup.bind("<Escape>", lambda e: self.on_popup_close())
   571	
   572	        btns = tk.Frame(popup, bg=theme.BG)
   573	        btns.pack(pady=10)
   574	        tk.Button(btns, text=t("btn.add"), command=do_add,
   575	                  bg=theme.GREEN, fg="white", width=10,
   576	                  font=self.font(10, "bold"), relief="flat"
   577	                  ).pack(side=i18n.side_start(), padx=5)
   578	        tk.Button(btns, text=t("btn.cancel"), command=self.on_popup_close,
   579	                  bg=theme.GRAY, fg="white", width=10,
   580	                  font=self.font(10, "bold"), relief="flat"
   581	                  ).pack(side=i18n.side_start(), padx=5)
   582	
   583	    def show_gram_popup(self, product):
   584	        price_kg, buy_kg = product[5], product[4]
   585	
   586	        def compute(qty, unit):
   587	            if unit == "g":
   588	                return dict(pieces_qty=qty, display_qty=qty, unit_display="g",
   589	                            total=(qty / 1000) * price_kg,
   590	                            profit=(qty / 1000) * (price_kg - buy_kg))
   591	            return dict(pieces_qty=qty * 1000, display_qty=qty,
   592	                        unit_display="kg",
   593	                        total=qty * price_kg,
   594	                        profit=qty * (price_kg - buy_kg))
   595	
   596	        self._quantity_popup(
   597	            product,
   598	            title=t("sales.popup_weight_title"),
   599	            header_lines=[product[2], t("sales.price_per_kg", p=price_kg)],
   600	            unit_options=[(t("sales.opt_grams"), "g"),
   601	                          (t("sales.opt_kilograms"), "kg")],
   602	            default_unit="g",
   603	            compute=compute,
   604	        )
   605	
   606	    def show_pack_popup(self, product):
   607	        ppp = product[9] or 1
   608	        price_pack = product[5]
   609	        price_unit = product[8] if product[8] > 0 else price_pack / ppp
   610	        buy_pack = product[4]
   611	        buy_unit = buy_pack / ppp
   612	
   613	        def compute(qty, unit):
   614	            if unit == "pack":
   615	                return dict(pieces_qty=qty * ppp, display_qty=qty,
   616	                            unit_display="pack",
   617	                            total=qty * price_pack,
   618	                            profit=qty * (price_pack - buy_pack))
   619	            return dict(pieces_qty=qty, display_qty=qty,
   620	                        unit_display="unité",
   621	                        total=qty * price_unit,
   622	                        profit=qty * (price_unit - buy_unit))
   623	
   624	        self._quantity_popup(
   625	            product,
   626	            title=t("sales.popup_pack_title"),
   627	            header_lines=[product[2],
   628	                          t("sales.pack_price", p=price_pack),
   629	                          t("sales.unit_price", p=price_unit)],
   630	            unit_options=[(t("sales.opt_packs"), "pack"),
   631	                          (t("sales.opt_units"), "unité")],
   632	            default_unit="unité",
   633	            compute=compute,
   634	        )
   635	
   636	    def show_cigarette_popup(self, product):
   637	        PACK = 20
   638	        stock_cig = int(product[7])
   639	        pack_price = product[5]
   640	        single_price = (product[11]
   641	                        if len(product) > 11 and product[11] > 0
   642	                        else pack_price / PACK)
   643	        buy_pack = product[4]
   644	        single_cost = (product[11]
   645	                       if len(product) > 11 and product[11] > 0
   646	                       else buy_pack / PACK)
   647	
   648	        def compute(qty, unit):
   649	            if unit == "pack":
   650	                return dict(pieces_qty=qty * PACK, display_qty=qty,
   651	                            unit_display="pack",
   652	                            total=qty * pack_price,
   653	                            profit=qty * (pack_price - buy_pack))
   654	            return dict(pieces_qty=qty, display_qty=qty,
   655	                        unit_display="unité",
   656	                        total=qty * single_price,
   657	                        profit=qty * (single_price - single_cost))
   658	
   659	        def stock_check(qty, unit):
   660	            needed = qty * PACK if unit == "pack" else qty
   661	            if needed > stock_cig:
   662	                return t("msg.stock_insufficient", available=stock_cig)
   663	            return None
   664	
   665	        self._quantity_popup(
   666	            product,
   667	            title=t("sales.popup_cigarette_title"),
   668	            header_lines=[product[2],
   669	                          t("sales.stock_cigarettes", n=stock_cig),
   670	                          t("sales.pack_price", p=pack_price),
   671	                          t("sales.unit_price", p=single_price)],
   672	            unit_options=[(t("sales.opt_packs"), "pack"),
   673	                          (t("sales.opt_units"), "unité")],
   674	            default_unit="unité",
   675	            compute=compute,
   676	            stock_check=stock_check,
   677	        )
   678	
   679	    def on_popup_close(self):
   680	        if self.active_popup and self.active_popup.winfo_exists():
   681	            self.active_popup.destroy()
   682	        self.active_popup = None
   683	        self.set_state("SCAN")
   684	        self.setup_keyboard_shortcuts()
   685	        self.barcode_entry.focus_set()
   686	
   687	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   688	    # Cart
   689	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   690	    def add_telecom_to_cart(self, name):
   691	        full_name = name + " DT"
   692	        product = self.telecom_by_name.get(full_name)
   693	        if not product:
   694	            self.show_notification(t("sales.not_found_full", name=full_name),
   695	                                   is_error=True)
   696	            return
   697	        full_product = (product[0], None, product[1], None,
   698	                        product[2], product[3], "piece", 999999,
   699	                        0, 1, "telecom", 0)
   700	        ok = self.add_to_cart(full_product, 1, "piece", 1,
   701	                              product[3], product[3] - product[2])
   702	        if ok:
   703	            self.show_notification(t("sales.added", name=product[1]))
   704	        self.barcode_entry.focus_set()
   705	
   706	    def add_to_cart(self, product, pieces_qty, unit_display, display_qty,
   707	                    price, profit):
   708	        ptype = product[10] if len(product) > 10 else "normal"
   709	        is_telecom = ptype == "telecom"
   710	        if not is_telecom and product[7] < pieces_qty:
   711	            self.show_notification(t("msg.stock_only", stock=product[7]),
   712	                                   is_error=True)
   713	            return False
   714	
   715	        for i, (p, q, tot, pr, u, dq) in enumerate(self.cart):
   716	            if p[0] == product[0] and u == unit_display:
   717	                new_pieces = q + pieces_qty
   718	                if not is_telecom and product[7] < new_pieces:
   719	                    self.show_notification(t("msg.stock_only", stock=product[7]),
   720	                                           is_error=True)
   721	                    return False
   722	                self.cart[i] = (p, new_pieces, tot + price, pr + profit,
   723	                                u, dq + display_qty)
   724	                self.update_cart()
   725	                return True
   726	
   727	        self.cart.append((product, pieces_qty, price, profit,
   728	                          unit_display, display_qty))
   729	        self.update_cart()
   730	        return True
   731	
   732	    def remove_from_cart(self):
   733	        sel = self.cart_tree.selection()
   734	        if not sel:
   735	            return
   736	        idx = self.cart_tree.index(sel[0])
   737	        if 0 <= idx < len(self.cart):
   738	            self.cart.pop(idx)
   739	            self.update_cart()
   740	            self.barcode_entry.focus_set()
   741	
   742	    def clear_cart(self):
   743	        self.cart = []
   744	        self.update_cart()
   745	        self.barcode_entry.focus_set()
   746	
   747	    def update_cart(self):
   748	        for row in self.cart_tree.get_children():
   749	            self.cart_tree.delete(row)
   750	        total = 0.0
   751	        for product, _p, line_total, _pr, unit, dq in self.cart:
   752	            unit_price = line_total / dq if dq > 0 else line_total
   753	            self.cart_tree.insert("", "end", values=(
   754	                product[2][:32], f"{dq:g}", unit,
   755	                f"{unit_price:.2f}", f"{line_total:.2f}",
   756	            ))
   757	            total += line_total
   758	        self.total_label.config(text=f"{total:.2f} DT")
   759	
   760	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   761	    # Checkout
   762	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   763	    def complete_sale(self, event=None):
   764	        if not auth.can(self.user, "sell"):
   765	            messagebox.showerror(t("msg.access_denied"), t("msg.no_permission"))
   766	            return
   767	        if not self.cart:
   768	            self.show_notification(t("sales.cart_empty"), is_error=True)
   769	            return
   770	        for product, pieces_qty, *_ in self.cart:
   771	            ptype = product[10] if len(product) > 10 else "normal"
   772	            if ptype == "telecom":
   773	                continue
   774	            if product[7] < pieces_qty:
   775	                self.show_notification(
   776	                    t("msg.stock_insufficient", available=product[7]),
   777	                    is_error=True)
   778	                return
   779	
   780	        stamp = datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")
   781	        total = 0.0
   782	        try:
   783	            for product, pieces_qty, line_total, profit, _u, _dq in self.cart:
   784	                db.add_sale(product[0], pieces_qty, profit, line_total, date=stamp)
   785	                total += line_total
   786	        except Exception as e:
   787	            messagebox.showerror(t("msg.error"), t("sales.save_error", e=e))
   788	            return
   789	        backup.do_backup()
   790	        self.show_notification(t("sales.cash_in", total=total))
   791	        self.clear_cart()
   792	
   793	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   794	    # Notifications
   795	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   796	    def show_notification(self, message, is_error=False):
   797	        if self.notification_label and self.notification_label.winfo_exists():
   798	            self.notification_label.destroy()
   799	        color = theme.RED if is_error else theme.GREEN
   800	        self.notification_label = tk.Label(self.notification_frame, text=message,
   801	                                           bg=color, fg="white",
   802	                                           font=self.font(9))
   803	        self.notification_label.pack(fill="both", expand=True)
   804	        self.frame.after(2200, self.hide_notification)
   805	
   806	    def hide_notification(self):
   807	        if self.notification_label and self.notification_label.winfo_exists():
   808	            self.notification_label.destroy()
   809	        self.notification_label = None
   810	
   811	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   812	    def on_show(self):
   813	        self.load_telecom_products()
   814	        self.set_state("SCAN")
   815	        self.setup_keyboard_shortcuts()
   816	        self.barcode_entry.focus_set()
   817	
   818	    def on_hide(self):
   819	        self._unbind_shortcuts()
   820	        if self.active_popup and self.active_popup.winfo_exists():
   821	            self.active_popup.destroy()
   822	        self.active_popup = None

    23	                          lambda: self.switch("sales")))

    29	                        lambda: self.switch("deleted")))

    87	                           font=self.font(10)).pack(side=i18n.side_start())

    89	        tk.Button(parent, text=t("users.create"), bg=theme.GREEN, fg="white",
    90	                  font=self.font(10, "bold"), width=12, relief="flat",
    91	                  command=self.create_user).pack(pady=18)

    93	    # --- listing --------------------------------------------------------
    94	    def _build_listing(self, parent):
    95	        container = tk.Frame(parent, bg=theme.BG_CARD)
    96	        container.pack(fill="both", expand=True, padx=10, pady=10)
    97	
    98	        columns = ("id", "username", "full_name", "role", "active", "created")
    99	        headings = [t("users.col_id"), t("users.col_username"),
   100	                    t("users.col_full_name"), t("users.col_role"),
   101	                    t("users.col_active"), t("users.col_created")]
   102	        widths = [45, 140, 180, 100, 70, 140]
   103	        self.tree = ttk.Treeview(container, columns=columns,
   104	                                 show="headings", height=20)
   105	        for col, hdr, w in zip(columns, headings, widths):
   106	            self.tree.heading(col, text=hdr)
   107	            self.tree.column(col, width=w,
   108	                             anchor=(i18n.anchor_start()
   109	                                     if col == "full_name" else "center"))
   110	        v = ttk.Scrollbar(container, orient="vertical", command=self.tree.yview)
   111	        self.tree.configure(yscrollcommand=v.set)
   112	        self.tree.pack(side=i18n.side_start(), fill="both", expand=True)
   113	        v.pack(side=i18n.side_end(), fill="y")
   114	
   115	        footer = tk.Frame(parent, bg=theme.BG_CARD)
   116	        footer.pack(fill="x", padx=10, pady=(0, 10))
   117	        for text_key, color, cmd in (
   118	            ("users.rename", theme.PURPLE, self.rename_dialog),
   119	            ("users.toggle_active", theme.ORANGE, self.toggle_active),
   120	            ("users.change_password", theme.BLUE, self.change_password_dialog),
   121	            ("users.delete", theme.RED, self.delete_selected),
   122	        ):
   123	            tk.Button(footer, text=t(text_key), bg=color, fg="white",
   124	                      font=self.font(10, "bold"), relief="flat",
   125	                      command=cmd).pack(side=i18n.side_start(), padx=3)
   126	        tk.Label(footer, text=t("users.warn_owner"), bg=theme.BG_CARD,
   127	                 fg=theme.ORANGE, font=self.font(9)
   128	                 ).pack(side=i18n.side_end(), padx=5)
   129	
   130	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   131	    def _selected_user(self):
   132	        sel = self.tree.selection()
   133	        if not sel:
   134	            messagebox.showwarning(t("msg.warning"), t("users.select_user"))
   135	            return None
   136	        return self.tree.item(sel[0])["values"]
   137	
   138	    def create_user(self):
   139	        username = self.entries["username"].get().strip()
   140	        full_name = self.entries["full_name"].get().strip()
   141	        password = self.entries["password"].get()
   142	        password2 = self.entries["password2"].get()
   143	        role = self.role_var.get()
   144	        if not username or not password:
   145	            messagebox.showwarning(t("msg.warning"), t("users.required_fields"))
   146	            return
   147	        if password != password2:
   148	            messagebox.showwarning(t("msg.warning"), t("users.password_mismatch"))
   149	            return
   150	        if len(password) < 4:
   151	            messagebox.showwarning(t("msg.warning"), t("users.password_short"))
   152	            return
   153	        try:
   154	            auth.create_user(username, password, role, full_name)
   155	            messagebox.showinfo(t("msg.success"),
   156	                                t("users.created_ok", u=username))
   157	            for e in self.entries.values():
   158	                e.delete(0, tk.END)
   159	            self.load_users()
   160	        except Exception as e:
   161	            messagebox.showerror(t("msg.error"), str(e))
   162	
   163	    def rename_dialog(self):
   164	        values = self._selected_user()
   165	        if not values:
   166	            return
   167	        user_id, old_username = int(values[0]), values[1]
   168	
   169	        dlg = tk.Toplevel(self.root)
   170	        dlg.title(t("users.rename_title", u=old_username))
   171	        dlg.geometry("360x180")
   172	        dlg.transient(self.root)
   173	        dlg.grab_set()
   174	        dlg.configure(bg=theme.BG)
   175	
   176	        tk.Label(dlg, text=t("users.new_name_for", u=old_username),
   177	                 bg=theme.BG, fg=theme.FG, font=self.font(10)
   178	                 ).pack(pady=(20, 4))
   179	        entry = tk.Entry(dlg, font=self.font(10), width=26,
   180	                         bg="white", fg=theme.FG, relief="solid", bd=1,
   181	                         justify=i18n.justify_text())
   182	        entry.pack(pady=6, ipady=3)
   183	        entry.insert(0, old_username)
   184	        entry.focus_set()
   185	        entry.select_range(0, tk.END)
   186	
   187	        def submit(event=None):
   188	            new_name = entry.get().strip()
   189	            if not new_name:
   190	                messagebox.showerror(t("msg.error"), t("users.name_empty"),
   191	                                     parent=dlg)
   192	                return
   193	            if new_name == old_username:
   194	                dlg.destroy()
   195	                return
   196	            try:
   197	                auth.rename_user(user_id, new_name)
   198	            except ValueError as e:
   199	                key = "err.rename_empty" if str(e) == "empty" else "err.rename_taken"
   200	                messagebox.showerror(
   201	                    t("msg.error"),
   202	                    t(key, name=new_name) if key.endswith("taken") else t(key),
   203	                    parent=dlg)
   204	                return
   205	            if self.user and self.user.get("id") == user_id:
   206	                self.user["username"] = new_name
   207	                try:
   208	                    self.root.title(t("app.window_title",
   209	                                       username=new_name,
   210	                                       role=t(f"role.{self.user['role']}")))
   211	                except tk.TclError:
   212	                    pass
   213	            messagebox.showinfo(t("msg.success"),
   214	                                t("users.renamed_ok", u=new_name), parent=dlg)
   215	            dlg.destroy()
   216	            self.load_users()
   217	
   218	        tk.Button(dlg, text=t("btn.save"), bg=theme.GREEN, fg="white",
   219	                  font=self.font(10, "bold"), width=10, relief="flat",
   220	                  command=submit).pack(pady=12)
   221	        dlg.bind("<Return>", submit)
   222	        dlg.bind("<KP_Enter>", submit)
   223	
   224	    def toggle_active(self):
   225	        values = self._selected_user()
   226	        if not values:
   227	            return
   228	        user_id = int(values[0])
   229	        if user_id == self.user["id"]:
   230	            messagebox.showerror(t("msg.error"), t("users.cannot_disable_self"))
   231	            return
   232	        is_active = values[4] == t("users.active_yes")
   233	        try:
   234	            auth.set_user_active(user_id, not is_active)
   235	            self.load_users()
   236	        except Exception as e:
   237	            messagebox.showerror(t("msg.error"), str(e))
   238	
   239	    def change_password_dialog(self):
   240	        values = self._selected_user()
   241	        if not values:
   242	            return
   243	        user_id, username = int(values[0]), values[1]
   244	
   245	        dlg = tk.Toplevel(self.root)
   246	        dlg.title(f"{t('users.change_password')} - {username}")
   247	        dlg.geometry("320x180")
   248	        dlg.transient(self.root)
   249	        dlg.grab_set()
   250	        dlg.configure(bg=theme.BG)
   251	
   252	        tk.Label(dlg, text=t("users.password_new"), bg=theme.BG,
   253	                 font=self.font(10)).pack(pady=(20, 4))
   254	        p1 = tk.Entry(dlg, show="*", font=self.font(10), width=22,
   255	                      justify=i18n.justify_text())
   256	        p1.pack(pady=4)
   257	        p1.focus_set()
   258	        tk.Label(dlg, text=t("users.confirm"), bg=theme.BG,
   259	                 font=self.font(10)).pack(pady=(4, 4))
   260	        p2 = tk.Entry(dlg, show="*", font=self.font(10), width=22,
   261	                      justify=i18n.justify_text())
   262	        p2.pack(pady=4)
   263	
   264	        def submit():
   265	            if p1.get() != p2.get():
   266	                messagebox.showerror(t("msg.error"), t("users.password_mismatch"),
   267	                                     parent=dlg)
   268	                return
   269	            if len(p1.get()) < 4:
   270	                messagebox.showerror(t("msg.error"), t("users.password_short"),
   271	                                     parent=dlg)
   272	                return
   273	            try:
   274	                auth.change_password(user_id, p1.get())
   275	                messagebox.showinfo(t("msg.success"),
   276	                                    t("users.password_changed"), parent=dlg)
   277	                dlg.destroy()
   278	            except Exception as e:
   279	                messagebox.showerror(t("msg.error"), str(e), parent=dlg)
   280	
   281	        tk.Button(dlg, text=t("btn.save"), bg=theme.GREEN, fg="white",
   282	                  font=self.font(10, "bold"), width=10, relief="flat",
   283	                  command=submit).pack(pady=12)
   284	        dlg.bind("<Return>", lambda e: submit())
   285	
   286	    def delete_selected(self):
   287	        values = self._selected_user()
   288	        if not values:
   289	            return
   290	        user_id, username = int(values[0]), values[1]
   291	        if user_id == self.user["id"]:
   292	            messagebox.showerror(t("msg.error"), t("users.cannot_delete_self"))
   293	            return
   294	        if not messagebox.askyesno(t("msg.confirmation"),
   295	                                   t("users.confirm_delete", u=username)):
   296	            return
   297	        try:
   298	            auth.delete_user(user_id)
   299	            self.load_users()
   300	        except Exception as e:
   301	            messagebox.showerror(t("msg.error"), str(e))
   302	
   303	    def load_users(self):
   304	        for row in self.tree.get_children():
   305	            self.tree.delete(row)
   306	        yes, no = t("users.active_yes"), t("users.active_no")
   307	        for uid, username, full_name, role, is_active, created in auth.list_users():
   308	            self.tree.insert("", "end", values=(
   309	                uid, username, full_name or "", t(f"role.{role}"),
   310	                yes if is_active else no, created or "",
   311	            ))
   312	
   313	    def on_show(self):
   314	        self.load_users()

     1	"""Reusable widgets: build_topbar, DateRangeFilter, ScrollableTreeview."""
     2	import tkinter as tk
     3	from datetime import datetime
     4	from tkinter import ttk
     5	
     6	from app import theme
     7	from utils import i18n
     8	from utils.i18n import t
     9	
    10	
    11	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    12	# Topbar
    13	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    14	def build_topbar(parent, *, color, title_key, buttons_start=(),
    15	                 buttons_end=(), logout_callback=None):
    16	    """Standard 55px topbar: start buttons | title | end buttons | logout."""
    17	    top = tk.Frame(parent, bg=color, height=theme.TOPBAR_H)
    18	    top.pack(fill="x")
    19	    top.pack_propagate(False)
    20	
    21	    top.grid_rowconfigure(0, weight=1)
    22	    top.grid_columnconfigure(0, weight=1)
    23	    top.grid_columnconfigure(1, weight=0)
    24	    top.grid_columnconfigure(2, weight=1)
    25	
    26	    if i18n.is_rtl():
    27	        start_col, end_col = 2, 0
    28	        start_sticky, end_sticky = "e", "w"
    29	    else:
    30	        start_col, end_col = 0, 2
    31	        start_sticky, end_sticky = "w", "e"
    32	
    33	    # --- start side ---
    34	    start = tk.Frame(top, bg=color)
    35	    start.grid(row=0, column=start_col, sticky=start_sticky, padx=8, pady=8)
    36	    for text, bg, cmd in buttons_start:
    37	        tk.Button(start, text=text, bg=bg, fg="white",
    38	                  font=i18n.ui_font(10), width=11, relief="flat",
    39	                  command=cmd).pack(side=i18n.side_start(), padx=3)
    40	
    41	    # --- title ---
    42	    tk.Label(top, text=t(title_key), bg=color, fg="white",
    43	             font=i18n.ui_font(13, "bold")
    44	             ).grid(row=0, column=1, padx=20)
    45	
    46	    # --- end side ---
    47	    end = tk.Frame(top, bg=color)
    48	    end.grid(row=0, column=end_col, sticky=end_sticky, padx=8, pady=8)
    49	    for text, bg, cmd in buttons_end:
    50	        tk.Button(end, text=text, bg=bg, fg="white",
    51	                  font=i18n.ui_font(10), width=11, relief="flat",
    52	                  command=cmd).pack(side=i18n.side_end(), padx=3)
    53	
    54	    # --- logout (always outermost on the end side) ---
    55	    if logout_callback:
    56	        tk.Button(end, text=t("logout.button"), bg=theme.LOGOUT, fg="white",
    57	                  font=i18n.ui_font(9), width=8, relief="flat",
    58	                  command=logout_callback
    59	                  ).pack(side=i18n.side_end(), padx=3)
    60	
    61	    return top
    62	
    63	
    64	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    65	# Date range filter
    66	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    67	class DateRangeFilter(tk.Frame):
    68	    """From [date] [time]  To [date] [time]  [Filter] [Clear] [↻]"""
    69	
    70	    def __init__(self, parent, *, bg=theme.BG_CARD,
    71	                 from_key="lbl.from", to_key="lbl.to",
    72	                 on_apply=None, on_clear=None, on_refresh=None,
    73	                 show_verify=False, on_verify=None):
    74	        super().__init__(parent, bg=bg, relief="groove", bd=1)
    75	        self._bg = bg
    76	        self._on_apply = on_apply
    77	        today = datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d")
    78	
    79	        # --- From ---
    80	        f = tk.Frame(self, bg=bg)
    81	        f.pack(side=i18n.side_start(), padx=10, pady=6)
    82	        tk.Label(f, text=t(from_key), bg=bg,
    83	                 font=i18n.ui_font(9)).pack(side=i18n.side_start())
    84	        self.start_date = self._entry(f, 10, today)
    85	        self.start_time = self._entry(f, 5, "00:00")
    86	
    87	        # --- To ---
    88	        e = tk.Frame(self, bg=bg)
    89	        e.pack(side=i18n.side_start(), padx=10, pady=6)
    90	        tk.Label(e, text=t(to_key), bg=bg,
    91	                 font=i18n.ui_font(9)).pack(side=i18n.side_start())
    92	        self.end_date = self._entry(e, 10, today)
    93	        self.end_time = self._entry(e, 5, "23:59")
    94	
    95	        # --- Actions ---
    96	        tk.Button(self, text=t("btn.filter"), bg=theme.BLUE, fg="white",
    97	                  font=i18n.ui_font(9), command=self._apply
    98	                  ).pack(side=i18n.side_start(), padx=5)
    99	        tk.Button(self, text=t("btn.clear_filter"), bg=theme.GRAY, fg="white",
   100	                  font=i18n.ui_font(9), command=self._clear
   101	                  ).pack(side=i18n.side_start(), padx=5)
   102	        if on_refresh:
   103	            tk.Button(self, text=t("btn.refresh"), bg=theme.BLUE, fg="white",
   104	                      font=i18n.ui_font(9), width=2, command=on_refresh
   105	                      ).pack(side=i18n.side_start(), padx=5)
   106	        if show_verify and on_verify:
   107	            tk.Button(self, text=t("btn.verify"), bg=theme.ORANGE, fg="white",
   108	                      font=i18n.ui_font(9), command=on_verify
   109	                      ).pack(side=i18n.side_start(), padx=5)
   110	
   111	    # --- helpers -------------------------------------------------------
   112	    def _entry(self, parent, width, default):
   113	        e = tk.Entry(parent, width=width, font=i18n.ui_font(9),
   114	                     justify=i18n.justify_text())
   115	        e.pack(side=i18n.side_start(), padx=2)
   116	        e.insert(0, default)
   117	        return e
   118	
   119	    def _apply(self):
   120	        try:
   121	            self.get_range()
   122	        except ValueError as msg:
   123	            from tkinter import messagebox
   124	            messagebox.showerror(t("msg.error"), str(msg))
   125	            return
   126	        if self._on_apply:
   127	            self._on_apply()
   128	
   129	    def _clear(self):
   130	        today = datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d")
   131	        for e, v in ((self.start_date, today), (self.start_time, "00:00"),
   132	                     (self.end_date, today), (self.end_time, "23:59")):
   133	            e.delete(0, tk.END)
   134	            e.insert(0, v)
   135	        if self._on_clear:
   136	            self._on_clear()
   137	        elif self._on_apply:
   138	            self._on_apply()
   139	
   140	    # --- public API ----------------------------------------------------
   141	    def get_range(self) -> tuple[str, str]:
   142	        """Return ("YYYY-MM-DD HH:MM:00", "YYYY-MM-DD HH:MM:59")."""
   143	        start = f"{self.start_date.get().strip()} {self.start_time.get().strip()}:00"
   144	        end = f"{self.end_date.get().strip()} {self.end_time.get().strip()}:59"
   145	        try:
   146	            datetime.strptime(start, "%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")
   147	            datetime.strptime(end, "%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")
   148	        except ValueError:
   149	            raise ValueError(t("reports.invalid_datetime"))
   150	        if start > end:
   151	            raise ValueError(t("reports.start_after_end"))
   152	        return start, end
   153	
   154	    def peek_range(self):
   155	        """Same as get_range but returns (None, None) on bad input."""
   156	        try:
   157	            return self.get_range()
   158	        except ValueError:
   159	            return None, None
   160	
   161	
   162	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   163	# Scrollable treeview
   164	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   165	class ScrollableTreeview(tk.Frame):
   166	    """Treeview + both scrollbars, correctly gridded."""
   167	
   168	    def __init__(self, parent, *, columns, headings, widths, height=20,
   169	                 anchors=None, selectmode="extended", style=None):
   170	        super().__init__(parent, bg=theme.BG_CARD, relief="groove", bd=1)
   171	        anchors = anchors or {}
   172	
   173	        self.tree = ttk.Treeview(self, columns=columns, show="headings",
   174	                                 selectmode=selectmode, height=height,
   175	                                 style=style or "Treeview")
   176	        for col, hdr, w in zip(columns, headings, widths):
   177	            self.tree.heading(col, text=hdr)
   178	            self.tree.column(col, width=w,
   179	                             anchor=anchors.get(col, "center"))
   180	
   181	        v = ttk.Scrollbar(self, orient="vertical", command=self.tree.yview)
   182	        h = ttk.Scrollbar(self, orient="horizontal", command=self.tree.xview)
   183	        self.tree.configure(yscrollcommand=v.set, xscrollcommand=h.set)
   184	
   185	        self.tree.grid(row=0, column=0, sticky="nsew")
   186	        v.grid(row=0, column=1, sticky="ns")
   187	        h.grid(row=1, column=0, sticky="ew")
   188	        self.grid_rowconfigure(0, weight=1)
   189	        self.grid_columnconfigure(0, weight=1)
   190	
   191	    def clear(self):
   192	        for row in self.tree.get_children():
   193	            self.tree.delete(row)
   194	
   195	    def insert_row(self, values, tags=()):
   196	        return self.tree.insert("", "end", values=values, tags=tags)
   197	
   198	    def selection_tags(self):
   199	        """Return the first tag of every selected row (usually the timestamp)."""
   200	        out = []
   201	        for item in self.tree.selection():
   202	            tags = self.tree.item(item, "tags")
   203	            if tags:
   204	                out.append(tags[0])
   205	        return out
   206	
   207	    def bind(self, event, handler):      # noqa: A003
   208	        self.tree.bind(event, handler)

FILE: scripts/check_locales.py
=================================================================

     1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
     2	"""Fail if any locale file is missing keys present in en.json."""
     3	import json
     4	import sys
     5	from pathlib import Path
     6	
     7	ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent / "locales"
     8	REFERENCE = "en.json"
     9	
    10	
    11	def load(name):
    12	    with open(ROOT / name, encoding="utf-8") as f:
    13	        return json.load(f)
    14	
    15	
    16	def main() -> int:
    17	    ref = load(REFERENCE)
    18	    ok = True
    19	    for path in sorted(ROOT.glob("*.json")):
    20	        if path.name == REFERENCE:
    21	            continue
    22	        data = load(path.name)
    23	        missing = set(ref) - set(data)
    24	        extra = set(data) - set(ref)
    25	        if missing:
    26	            ok = False
    27	            print(f"[{path.name}] missing {len(missing)} key(s):")
    28	            for k in sorted(missing):
    29	                print(f"    - {k}")
    30	        if extra:
    31	            print(f"[{path.name}] has {len(extra)} extra key(s) "
    32	                  f"(not in {REFERENCE}):")
    33	            for k in sorted(extra):
    34	                print(f"    + {k}")
    35	    if ok:
    36	        print("Locales OK.")
    37	    return 0 if ok else 1
    38	
    39	
    40	if __name__ == "__main__":
    41	    sys.exit(main())

     1	"""Users, roles, permissions, password hashing."""
     2	import hashlib
     3	import secrets
     4	
     5	from database.connection import get_connection
     6	
     7	
     8	PERMISSIONS = {
     9	    "owner": {"*"},
    10	    "cashier": {
    11	        "sell",
    12	        "view_reports",
    13	        "view_history",
    14	        "soft_delete_sale",
    15	    },
    16	}
    17	
    18	
    19	def hash_password(password: str) -> str:
    20	    salt = secrets.token_hex(16)
    21	    iterations = 200_000
    22	    dk = hashlib.pbkdf2_hmac(
    23	        "sha256", password.encode("utf-8"),
    24	        bytes.fromhex(salt), iterations,
    25	    )
    26	    return f"pbkdf2_sha256${iterations}${salt}${dk.hex()}"
    27	
    28	
    29	def verify_password(password: str, stored: str) -> bool:
    30	    try:
    31	        algo, iter_s, salt, hash_hex = stored.split("$")
    32	        if algo != "pbkdf2_sha256":
    33	            return False
    34	        dk = hashlib.pbkdf2_hmac(
    35	            "sha256", password.encode("utf-8"),
    36	            bytes.fromhex(salt), int(iter_s),
    37	        )
    38	        return secrets.compare_digest(dk.hex(), hash_hex)
    39	    except Exception:
    40	        return False
    41	
    42	
    43	def _create_user(conn, username, password, role, full_name=""):
    44	    """Internal — used by migrations to seed the owner."""
    45	    if role not in PERMISSIONS:
    46	        raise ValueError(f"Unknown role: {role}")
    47	    conn.execute(
    48	        "INSERT INTO users (username, password_hash, full_name, role) "
    49	        "VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)",
    50	        (username, hash_password(password), full_name, role),
    51	    )
    52	
    53	
    54	def create_user(username, password, role, full_name=""):
    55	    conn = get_connection()
    56	    try:
    57	        _create_user(conn, username, password, role, full_name)
    58	        conn.commit()
    59	    finally:
    60	        conn.close()
    61	
    62	
    63	def authenticate(username: str, password: str):
    64	    conn = get_connection()
    65	    try:
    66	        cur = conn.cursor()
    67	        cur.execute(
    68	            "SELECT id, username, password_hash, full_name, role, is_active "
    69	            "FROM users WHERE username = ?",
    70	            (username,),
    71	        )
    72	        row = cur.fetchone()
    73	        if not row:
    74	            return None
    75	        user_id, uname, pwd_hash, full_name, role, is_active = row
    76	        if not is_active or not verify_password(password, pwd_hash):
    77	            return None
    78	        return {"id": user_id, "username": uname,
    79	                "full_name": full_name, "role": role}
    80	    finally:
    81	        conn.close()
    82	
    83	
    84	def list_users():
    85	    conn = get_connection()
    86	    try:
    87	        cur = conn.cursor()
    88	        cur.execute(
    89	            "SELECT id, username, full_name, role, is_active, created_at "
    90	            "FROM users ORDER BY role, username"
    91	        )
    92	        return cur.fetchall()
    93	    finally:
    94	        conn.close()
    95	
    96	
    97	def set_user_active(user_id, active: bool):
    98	    conn = get_connection()
    99	    try:
   100	        conn.execute("UPDATE users SET is_active = ? WHERE id = ?",
   101	                     (1 if active else 0, user_id))
   102	        conn.commit()
   103	    finally:
   104	        conn.close()
   105	
   106	
   107	def change_password(user_id, new_password: str):
   108	    conn = get_connection()
   109	    try:
   110	        conn.execute("UPDATE users SET password_hash = ? WHERE id = ?",
   111	                     (hash_password(new_password), user_id))
   112	        conn.commit()
   113	    finally:
   114	        conn.close()
   115	
   116	
   117	def rename_user(user_id, new_username: str):
   118	    new_username = new_username.strip()
   119	    if not new_username:
   120	        raise ValueError("empty")
   121	    conn = get_connection()
   122	    try:
   123	        cur = conn.cursor()
   124	        cur.execute("SELECT id FROM users WHERE username = ? AND id != ?",
   125	                    (new_username, user_id))
   126	        if cur.fetchone():
   127	            raise ValueError("taken")
   128	        cur.execute("UPDATE users SET username = ? WHERE id = ?",
   129	                    (new_username, user_id))
   130	        conn.commit()
   131	    finally:
   132	        conn.close()
   133	
   134	
   135	def delete_user(user_id):
   136	    conn = get_connection()
   137	    try:
   138	        conn.execute("DELETE FROM users WHERE id = ?", (user_id,))
   139	        conn.commit()
   140	    finally:
   141	        conn.close()
   142	
   143	
   144	def can(user, permission: str) -> bool:
   145	    if not user:
   146	        return False
   147	    perms = PERMISSIONS.get(user.get("role"), set())
   148	    return "*" in perms or permission in perms

FILE: utils/backup.py
=================================================================

     1	"""Backup: hourly, on exit, and after each sale. Keeps last 10."""
     2	import os
     3	import shutil
     4	import threading
     5	import time
     6	from datetime import datetime
     7	
     8	DB_PATH = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(__file__)), "stock.db")
     9	BACKUP_DIR = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(__file__)), "backups")
    10	
    11	_KEEP = 10
    12	_PREFIX = "auto_backup_"
    13	
    14	
    15	def do_backup() -> bool:
    16	    os.makedirs(BACKUP_DIR, exist_ok=True)
    17	    if not os.path.exists(DB_PATH):
    18	        return False
    19	
    20	    stamp = datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S")
    21	    dest = os.path.join(BACKUP_DIR, f"{_PREFIX}{stamp}.db")
    22	    shutil.copy2(DB_PATH, dest)
    23	
    24	    backups = sorted(f for f in os.listdir(BACKUP_DIR)
    25	                     if f.startswith(_PREFIX))
    26	    for old in backups[:-_KEEP]:
    27	        os.remove(os.path.join(BACKUP_DIR, old))
    28	    return True
    29	
    30	
    31	def _loop():
    32	    while True:
    33	        time.sleep(3600)
    34	        try:
    35	            do_backup()
    36	        except Exception as e:
    37	            print(f"Hourly backup failed: {e}")
    38	
    39	
    40	def start_auto_backup() -> None:
    41	    os.makedirs(BACKUP_DIR, exist_ok=True)
    42	    threading.Thread(target=_loop, daemon=True).start()
=================================================================
FILE: utils/i18n.py
=================================================================

     1	"""Translation system with RTL + Arabic-shaping helpers.
     2	
     3	All user-facing strings live in `locales/<lang>.json`. Screens must call
     4	`t("some.key")` and never hard-code text.
     5	"""
     6	import json
     7	import os
     8	from pathlib import Path
     9	
    10	try:
    11	    import arabic_reshaper
    12	    from bidi.algorithm import get_display as _bidi_display
    13	    _HAS_ARABIC_SHAPING = True
    14	except ImportError:
    15	    _HAS_ARABIC_SHAPING = False
    16	    _bidi_display = None
    17	    arabic_reshaper = None
    18	
    19	
    20	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    21	# Config
    22	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    23	_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
    24	_LOCALES_DIR = _ROOT / "locales"
    25	CONFIG_FILE = _ROOT / "settings.json"
    26	
    27	LANGUAGES = {
    28	    "en": "English",
    29	    "fr": "Français",
    30	    "ar": "العربية",
    31	}
    32	RTL_LANGUAGES = {"ar"}
    33	
    34	_current = "fr"
    35	_CACHE: dict[str, dict] = {}
    36	
    37	
    38	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    39	# RTL / layout helpers — used by every screen
    40	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    41	def is_rtl() -> bool:
    42	    return _current in RTL_LANGUAGES
    43	
    44	def side_start() -> str:  return "right" if is_rtl() else "left"
    45	def side_end()   -> str:  return "left"  if is_rtl() else "right"
    46	def anchor_start() -> str: return "e" if is_rtl() else "w"
    47	def anchor_end()   -> str: return "w" if is_rtl() else "e"
    48	def justify_text() -> str: return "right" if is_rtl() else "left"
    49	
    50	
    51	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    52	# Font selection
    53	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    54	_ARABIC_FONTS = (
    55	    "Noto Naskh Arabic", "Noto Sans Arabic", "Noto Sans",
    56	    "DejaVu Sans", "FreeSans", "Tahoma", "Segoe UI",
    57	    "Geeza Pro", "Arial Unicode MS", "Arial",
    58	)
    59	_LATIN_FONTS = ("Helvetica", "DejaVu Sans", "Arial")
    60	
    61	
    62	def _pick_font(candidates):
    63	    try:
    64	        import tkinter.font as tkfont
    65	        available = set(tkfont.families())
    66	    except Exception:
    67	        return candidates[0] if candidates else "TkDefaultFont"
    68	    for family in candidates:
    69	        if family in available:
    70	            return family
    71	    return candidates[-1] if candidates else "TkDefaultFont"
    72	
    73	
    74	def ui_font(size: int, weight: str = "normal"):
    75	    family = _pick_font(_ARABIC_FONTS if is_rtl() else _LATIN_FONTS)
    76	    return (family, size, weight) if weight != "normal" else (family, size)
    77	
    78	
    79	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    80	# Arabic shaping / bidi
    81	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    82	def _has_arabic(text: str) -> bool:
    83	    for ch in text:
    84	        if ("\u0600" <= ch <= "\u06FF" or "\u0750" <= ch <= "\u077F" or
    85	                "\uFB50" <= ch <= "\uFDFF" or "\uFE70" <= ch <= "\uFEFF"):
    86	            return True
    87	    return False
    88	
    89	
    90	def rtl_display(text):
    91	    """Visual-order, pre-shaped string for Tk on Linux. No-op elsewhere."""
    92	    if not text or not isinstance(text, str):
    93	        return text
    94	    if not _HAS_ARABIC_SHAPING or not _has_arabic(text):
    95	        return text
    96	    try:
    97	        return _bidi_display(arabic_reshaper.reshape(text))
    98	    except Exception:
    99	        return text
   100	
   101	
   102	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   103	# Config load / save
   104	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   105	def _load_config() -> None:
   106	    global _current
   107	    if not CONFIG_FILE.exists():
   108	        return
   109	    try:
   110	        with open(CONFIG_FILE, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
   111	            lang = json.load(f).get("language", "fr")
   112	            if lang in LANGUAGES:
   113	                _current = lang
   114	    except Exception:
   115	        pass
   116	
   117	
   118	def save_config(lang: str) -> None:
   119	    global _current
   120	    if lang not in LANGUAGES:
   121	        return
   122	    _current = lang
   123	    try:
   124	        with open(CONFIG_FILE, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
   125	            json.dump({"language": lang}, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
   126	    except Exception:
   127	        pass
   128	
   129	
   130	def get_language() -> str:
   131	    return _current
   132	
   133	
   134	def set_language(lang: str) -> None:
   135	    save_config(lang)
   136	
   137	
   138	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   139	# Translation
   140	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   141	def _load(lang: str) -> dict:
   142	    if lang not in _CACHE:
   143	        path = _LOCALES_DIR / f"{lang}.json"
   144	        try:
   145	            with open(path, encoding="utf-8") as f:
   146	                _CACHE[lang] = json.load(f)
   147	        except Exception:
   148	            _CACHE[lang] = {}
   149	    return _CACHE[lang]
   150	
   151	
   152	def t(key: str, **kwargs) -> str:
   153	    """Translate `key`, format with kwargs, apply RTL shaping."""
   154	    text = _load(_current).get(key)
   155	    if text is None:
   156	        text = _load("en").get(key, key)
   157	    if kwargs:
   158	        try:
   159	            text = text.format(**kwargs)
   160	        except Exception:
   161	            pass
   162	    return rtl_display(text)
   163	
   164	
   165	_load_config()
=================================================================
FILE: utils/last_report.py
=================================================================

     1	import os
     2	import json
     3	from datetime import datetime
     4	
     5	REPORTS_DIR = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(__file__)), 'reports')
     6	LAST_REPORT_FILE = os.path.join(REPORTS_DIR, 'last_report.json')
     7	
     8	def save_last_report(end_dt):
     9	    """Save the end datetime of the last generated report."""
    10	    data = {'last_end': end_dt}
    11	    if not os.path.exists(REPORTS_DIR):
    12	        os.makedirs(REPORTS_DIR)
    13	    with open(LAST_REPORT_FILE, 'w') as f:
    14	        json.dump(data, f)
    15	
    16	def load_last_report():
    17	    """Return the last end datetime string, or None if not exists."""
    18	    if os.path.exists(LAST_REPORT_FILE):
    19	        with open(LAST_REPORT_FILE, 'r') as f:
    20	            data = json.load(f)
    21	            return data.get('last_end')
    22	    return None
